### [100] [FlowMaps: Modeling Long-Term Multimodal Object Dynamics with Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20209)
*Francesco Argenziano,Miguel Saavedra-Ruiz,Sacha Morin,Charlie Gauthier,Daniele Nardi,Liam Paull*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowMaps是一种潜在流匹配模型，用于估计连续3D空间中动态物体未来位置的多模态分布，通过学习物体间的隐式依赖关系和时间演化规律，在动态物体导航任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人部署在家庭环境中需要联合理解3D场景的空间和时间动态，但物体因人类日常交互而频繁改变位置，使机器人难以将当前观测与之前见过的物体可靠关联。然而人类行为具有习惯性和规律性，物体位置呈现时空一致模式，机器人可以学习这些模式并用于导航等下游任务。

Method: 提出FlowMaps，一种潜在流匹配（latent flow matching）模型。该模型在连续3D空间中估计动态物体未来位置的多模态分布，学习物体间的隐式依赖关系及其时间演化，以过去的人类交互为条件预测物体位置的可能变化，并支持泛化到具有相似物体习惯的未见环境。

Result: 在超过600个episode的模拟和真实环境动态物体导航任务中，FlowMaps优于最先进的方法，证明了通过连续、多模态的时空分布建模物体动态能提升机器人在变化家庭环境中的搜索与导航能力。

Conclusion: 建模物体动态的连续多模态时空分布可以有效提升机器人在动态家居环境中的导航性能，FlowMaps通过潜在流匹配实现了对未来物体位置分布的有效估计和跨环境泛化。

Abstract: Joint spatial and temporal understanding of 3D scenes is a crucial requirement for robots deployed in everyday household environments. Such agents must not only comprehend and navigate spatial layouts, but also reason about how these spaces evolve over time. In particular, humans interact with objects daily, causing them to change position throughout the environment and making it difficult for robots to reliably associate current observations with previously seen objects. However, these interactions are not random: human habits and routines induce spatio-temporally consistent patterns in object locations, which robotic agents can potentially learn and then exploit for downstream tasks such as navigation. To this end, we introduce FlowMaps, a latent flow matching model for estimating multimodal distributions over the future locations of dynamic objects in a continuous 3D space. By learning the implicit dependencies among objects and their temporal evolution, FlowMaps predicts likely changes in object locations conditioned on past human interactions, while supporting generalization across previously unseen environments that share similar object routines. To demonstrate the utility of this method, we deploy FlowMaps in a downstream dynamic Object Navigation task in both simulated and real-world environments. Across more than 600 episodes, FlowMaps outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, showing that modeling object dynamics through continuous, multimodal spatio-temporal distributions improves robotic search and navigation in changing household environments. Code and additional material is available at https://fra-tsuna.github.io/flowmaps/.

</details>


### [101] [Mobile Target Search with Imperfect Perception: A Partially Observable Stochastic Game Theoretical Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20232)
*Hanzheng Zhang,Shu Liang,Shuyu Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对不完美感知下的移动目标搜索问题，采用部分可观测随机博弈方法，提出可检测性概念和基于聚合势博弈的分布式算法，并通过数值仿真验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器限制、恶意干扰或通信噪声会导致感知不完美（虚警和漏检），而目标具备智能逃逸能力，传统的POMDP方法无法建模这种对抗交互，因此需要更合适的理论框架。

Method: 采用部分可观测随机博弈(POSG)框架建模搜索者与智能目标之间的对抗交互；基于随机递归分析提出可检测性概念及充分判据；设计服务器辅助的分布式算法，利用聚合势博弈结构协调搜索者，并采用KL散度约简进行目标预测。

Result: 数值仿真验证了所提算法的有效性，并支持了可检测性分析的理论结论。

Conclusion: POSG框架能有效建模不完美感知下的目标搜索-逃逸对抗问题；提出的可检测性判据为搜索策略设计提供了理论保障；分布式算法在实际场景中具有可行性和有效性。

Abstract: This paper investigates mobile target search under imperfect perceptions caused by sensor limitations, malicious jamming, or communication noise. Searchers and targets operate in a grid-shaped area with bounded mobility, leading to a dynamic interplay between search and evasion. To capture this adversarial interaction under imperfect perceptions, we adopt the partially observable stochastic game (POSG) approach, which generalizes partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) by incorporating target intelligence. To handle false alarms and missed detections caused by perceptual uncertainties, we propose a novel detectability concept to determine whether a search strategy guarantees eventual detection, and provide sufficient detectability criteria based on stochastic recurrence analysis. We further develop a server-assisted distributed algorithm that utilizes the aggregative potential game structure for searchers and a KL-divergence-based reduction for target prediction. Numerical simulations validate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm and support the detectability analysis.

</details>


### [102] [Finetuning Vision-Language-Action Models Requires Fewer Layers Than You Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20246)
*Gia-Binh Nguyen,Trong-Bao Ho,Thien-Loc Ha,Khoa Vo,Philip Lund Møller,Quang T. Nguyen,Long Dinh,Tuan Dam,Vu Duong,Tung M. Luu,Trung Le,Tran Nguyen Le,Minh Vu,An Thai Le,Ngan Le,Daniel Sonntag,James Zou,Jan Peters,Duy M. H. Nguyen,Ngo Anh Vien*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发现VLA模型存在严重的层间表征冗余，提出了一种无需训练的压缩方法，通过CKA识别并移除冗余层，可将模型深度压缩50%，在微调和推理阶段分别带来40-50%和30%的加速，且性能不降甚至提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型参数量巨大（数十亿），在下游微调和实时推理中计算负担过重，亟需高效的模型压缩方法。

Method: 通过单次前向传递的Centered Kernel Alignment（CKA）识别冗余层特征，直接移除成对的冗余层，实现模型深度压缩最高50%，整个过程无需训练。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboCasa、SimplerEnv等仿真基准以及10个真实世界操作任务、4种机器人平台上，训练时间减少40-50%，推理速度提升30%，性能持平或超越原始全尺寸模型。

Conclusion: 先进VLA模型实际所需的层数远少于此前假设，本文提供了一种高效的计算范式，可扩展至大规模机器人学习。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models pre-trained on massive video-robot datasets have revolutionized robotic manipulation, yet their multi-billion parameter architectures impose prohibitive computational burdens during downstream fine-tuning and real-time inference. In this work, we reveal a highly non-trivial architectural characteristic of these continuous control foundation policies (e.g., pi_0, GR00T-N1.5): despite being trained on diverse physical trajectories, they exhibit severe layer-wise representational redundancy. To exploit this, we introduce a structural compression pipeline that is entirely training-free, bypassing the need of existing methods to load full-scale models to learn optimized token reductions or dynamic layer selectors. Instead, using only a single forward pass via Centered Kernel Alignment to identify redundant layer features, we remove twin layers to permanently compress the model depth by up to 50% across both the VLM backbone and the continuous control policy head. Downstream fine-tuning of this streamlined architecture yields a dual acceleration benefit: a 40-50% reduction in training time and up to 30% faster real-time inference, while matching or exceeding full-scale base model performance. We comprehensively validate our method across three simulation benchmarks (LIBERO, RoboCasa, SimplerEnv) and 10 diverse real-world manipulation tasks across 4 unique robotic embodiments. These results prove that advanced VLAs require significantly fewer layers than previously assumed, offering a highly compute-efficient paradigm for scalable robot learning.

</details>


### [103] [Efficiently Linking Real Scenes with Synthetic Data Generation for AI-based Cognitive Robotics and Computer Vision Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20272)
*Paul Koch,Vivek Chavan,André Sers,Adem Karakurt,Paul Hofmann,Mohamad Zaher Ziadeh,Jörg Krüger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文讨论了AI视觉模型在认知机器人领域的应用现状、挑战与趋势，重点分析了仿真与真实世界之间的域差距问题，并介绍了作者在训练数据生成方面连接两者的工作进行中研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI视觉模型驱动着认知机器人在工业和家庭场景中的潜在应用，但现有方法在训练数据、AI架构、精度限制和跨域可扩展性方面仍面临挑战，亟需更强大高效的方法来解决这些问题。

Method: 本文通过综述当前最先进方法的局限性与趋势，并介绍作者正在进行的链接仿真与真实世界训练数据生成的工作，以弥合域差距。

Result: 识别了当前AI视觉模型在认知机器人领域面临的主要挑战，包括精度限制、域差距和可扩展性问题，并提出了通过训练数据生成连接仿真与真实世界的解决方案思路。

Conclusion: 仿真与真实世界之间的域差距是当前认知机器人视觉模型的关键瓶颈，通过改进训练数据生成方法（链接仿真与现实）有潜力提升模型的泛化能力和实际应用效果。

Abstract: AI vision models are a driving factor for the potential use case scenarios of cognitive robotics within in the industry and household applications. A large array of methods from semantic environment analysis towards 6D and grasping pose estimation have been proposed based on the latest AI achievements. However, such advancements require further strong and efficient methods w.r.t. training data and AI-architectures, which are capable in synergy to tackle current challenges, precision limits, and scalability beyond domain gaps. In this paper, we discuss these current limits and trends in the related state-of-the-art which are challenging those. Further we discuss our current work in progress on bridging the domain gap between simulations and real world applications by linking those in the training data generation.

</details>


### [104] [Co-VLA: Coordination-Aware Structured Action Modeling for Dual-Arm Vision-Language-Action Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20285)
*Yandong Wang,Jiaqian Yu,Xiongfeng Peng,Lu Xu,Yamin Mao,Weiming Li,Jaewook Yoo,Dongwook Lee,Daehyun Ji,Mingbo Zhao,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Co-VLA 提出了一种通过结构化动作专家（SAE）和隐变量感知控制器（LAC）向VLA模型引入显式结构先验的协调感知双臂操作框架，在紧耦合任务中相比单块基线模型成功率提升27%，OOD场景性能翻倍，任务完成时间缩短25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在双臂操作中依赖隐式协调，但面对紧耦合任务和执行约束时，隐式协调无法保证可靠、可解释和稳定的行为，需要显式的结构先验来建模双臂协调。

Method: 将单块动作头替换为结构化动作专家（SAE），通过模块化的协调感知损失函数显式建模共享隐变量（编码任务级协调意图）和残差隐变量（编码各臂执行调整）；部署时使用隐变量感知控制器（LAC）实时调节同步强度、执行不对称性、平滑性和安全约束，在关节指令层面操作，兼容标准控制管线。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界基准测试中，Co-VLA在紧协调任务中成功率提升27%，OOD真实场景性能从13%提升至27%（翻倍以上），任务完成时间最高缩短25%。

Conclusion: 显式的协调结构先验能够有效增强VLA模型在双臂操作中的性能，尤其在紧耦合和OOD场景下效果显著，且不依赖力控或阻抗控制。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models show strong capabilities in single and dual-arm robotic manipulation. Prior works show coordinated bimanual behaviors can emerge from end-to-end learning, leveraging large vision-language backbones with continuous action prediction. However, as bimanual tasks become tightly coupled and execution constraints become critical, implicit coordination alone is insufficient to ensure reliable, interpretable, and stable behavior.
  In this work, we propose Co-VLA, a coordination-aware bimanual manipulation framework introducing explicit structural priors into VLA models. We instantiate our method on a state-of-the-art vision-language backbone by replacing its monolithic action head with a Structured Action Expert (SAE) designed for bimanual coordination. Specifically, we introduce explicit structure at the action generation level with a modular coordination-aware loss that shapes shared and residual latents according to task-specific structures. The shared latent encodes task-level coordination intent, while residual latents capture execution adjustments for each arm.
  At deployment, a Latent-Aware Controller (LAC) interprets the learned representations to modulate synchronization strength, execution asymmetry, smoothness, and safety constraints in real time. LAC operates at the joint-command level and remains compatible with standard control pipelines without requiring force or impedance control. Experiments across simulation and real-world benchmarks show Co-VLA significantly outperforms monolithic baselines, achieving a 27% success rate gain in tight-coordination tasks, more than doubling performance in OOD real-world scenarios (from 13% to 27%), and reducing task completion time by up to 25%.

</details>


### [105] [Towards 3D karst underwater scene reconstruction from rotating sonar data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20322)
*Georgios Evangelos Margaritis,Lionel Lapierre,Simon Rohou,Zhi Yan,Andreas Nüchter,François Goulette*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合连续时间SLAM轨迹校正和两阶段深度学习表面重建的管道，用于从声纳剖面仪数据重建水下岩溶管道，生成可导航的3D网格以支持水文地质分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 岩溶含水层提供关键淡水资源，但由于其复杂且难以理解的地下几何结构，存在显著危害。传统3D重建方法受限于声纳数据的稀疏性和噪声，以及导航估计的漂移问题，难以有效绘制这些环境。

Method: 提出一种管道：首先采用连续时间SLAM方法校正轨迹漂移，然后结合一种新颖的两阶段深度学习方法进行表面重建，最终生成沉浸式可导航的3D网格。

Result: 成功从稀疏且含噪的声纳剖面仪数据中重建出水下岩溶管道的3D结构，生成了可用于水文地质分析的沉浸式可导航3D网格。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了水下岩溶环境3D重建中的轨迹漂移和数据稀疏噪声问题，为水文地质分析提供了实用的重建管道。

Abstract: Karst aquifers provide critical freshwater resources but pose significant hazards due to their complex and poorly understood subsurface geometry. Mapping these environments is challenging because sonar data from underwater exploration is sparse and noisy, while navigation estimates suffer from drift limiting standard 3D reconstruction methods. We present a pipeline for reconstructing underwater karst conduits from a sonar profiler. We combine a continuous-time SLAM approach to correct trajectory drift with a novel two-stage deep learning method for surface reconstruction, producing an immersive and navigable 3D mesh for hydrogeological analysis.

</details>


### [106] [Autonomous Driving with Priority-Ordered STL Specifications Under Multimodal Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20336)
*Taha Bouzid,Shuhao Qi,Mircea Lazar,Sofie Haesaert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于信号时态逻辑（STL）词典排序的不确定性感知轨迹规划框架，结合模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制，在安全关键场景下有效处理冲突目标与多模态不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全关键场景下，自动驾驶轨迹规划常常无法同时满足所有需求（安全性、舒适性、交通规则等），需要根据重要性进行优先级排序，且需要显式考虑周围交通预测的不确定性。

Method: 提出不确定性感知的轨迹规划框架，采用STL规范上的预定义词典排序（lexicographic ordering），确保排序在不确定性下仍然有效，并结合MPPI控制实现该公式化方法。

Result: 在仿真场景中验证了该方法在现实多模态不确定性下有效处理冲突目标的能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够高效地在不确定性存在的情况下，依据优先级处理自动驾驶中相互冲突的轨迹规划需求。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles must plan trajectories that satisfy a multitude of requirements on safety, passenger comfort, and compliance with traffic rules. However, in safety-critical scenarios, it is not always possible to satisfy all requirements simultaneously, necessitating their prioritization based on importance. At the same time, in these safety-critical scenarios, the uncertainty in trajectory predictions of the surrounding traffic, such as other vehicles and pedestrians, should be explicitly accounted for. In this work, we propose an uncertainty-aware trajectory planning framework that incorporates a predefined lexicographic ordering over Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications that stays valid under uncertainty. We implement this formulation with Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control and we demonstrate the effectiveness of our method on simulation scenarios, showing that our framework efficiently handles conflicting objectives under realistic multi-modal uncertainty.

</details>


### [107] [An Infrastructure-less, Control-Independent Solution to Relative Localisation of a Team of Mobile Robots using Ranging Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20365)
*Paolo Golinelli,Tommaso Faraci,Daniele Fontanelli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种去中心化协作定位算法，仅依靠局部里程计、稀疏测距和短程通信，无需锚点或控制机器人运动，采用多假设贝叶斯框架处理不可观测条件，实现机器人团队的鲁棒定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无固定基础设施、需快速灵活部署且系统要求极简的场景下，现有机器人团队协作定位方法通常需要锚点、集中式计算或控制机器人运动以保证可观性，无法同时满足所有需求。

Method: 提出去中心化协作定位算法：无需锚点，完全去中心化；不要求控制机器人运动；仅依赖局部里程计、稀疏机器人间测距和短程通信；采用多假设贝叶斯框架维护所有可行解集合以应对瞬态不可观测条件；通过信息共享使每个智能体获益于整个团队的估计。

Result: 算法在部分连通条件下仍能工作，通过信息共享使每个机器人获得全局团队级估计，对瞬态不可观测条件具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法同时解决了无锚点、完全去中心化、无需控制机器人运动以保证可观性等多个挑战，适用范围广泛。

Abstract: The ability to localise teams of robots is essential for applications ranging from robotic fleets in unstructured environments to cooperative control and navigation tasks. In such contexts, fixed infrastructure is often unavailable, deployments must be fast and flexible, and system requirements must be minimal. We present a decentralised cooperative localisation algorithm that addresses all these challenges at once. The method is anchor-less, fully decentralised, and, unlike most existing approaches, does not require controlling the robots motion to ensure team observability. It relies only on local odometry, sparse inter-agent ranging measurements, and short-range communication, all of which are widely available in practice. The algorithm adopts a multi-hypothesis Bayesian framework that maintains the entire set of feasible solutions, ensuring robustness under transient unobservable conditions. Moreover, through information sharing, each agent benefits from the estimates of the entire group, even in partially connected conditions.

</details>


### [108] [CoLI: A Reproducible Platform for Continuum Robot Learning via Monolithic 3D Printing and Isomorphic Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20389)
*Ziyuan Tang,Chenxi Xiao**

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多材料3D打印的连续体机器人开源平台，通过简化制造流程、同构遥操作映射和模仿学习自主控制，解决了可重复性差和建模复杂的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人因制造装配复杂、运动学建模困难、缺乏直观控制界面而导致可重复性差，阻碍了其在实际和研究中的应用。

Method: 采用多材料3D打印实现单片式柔性结构制造；通过同构遥操作接口建立直接执行器级映射，避免显式运动学建模；支持基于模仿学习的自主控制。

Result: 硬件表征和操作任务实验验证了该平台的可重复性和学习就绪特性。

Conclusion: 该开源平台为连续体机器人社区提供了一个可重复、支持算法开发和系统基准测试的学习型机器人系统。

Abstract: Continuum robots offer strong potential for manipulation tasks due to their high degrees of freedom, compliant structures, and operational safety. However, their adoption in both research and practical applications has been hindered by reproducibility issues arising from complex fabrication and assembly processes, challenging kinematic modeling, and a lack of intuitive control interfaces. To address these challenges, we present a novel open-source continuum robot design. The platform features a simplified fabrication pipeline enabled by multi-material 3D printing, allowing the arm to be fabricated as a monolithic compliant structure with minimal assembly. Control is achieved through an isomorphic teleoperation interface that establishes a direct actuator-level mapping, eliminating the need for explicit kinematic modeling and providing a singularity-free mapping. Building on this hardware design, the platform further supports imitation-learning-based autonomous control. The proposed system is evaluated through hardware characterization and a set of manipulation tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that the platform provides a reproducible, learning-ready continuum robot system, accelerating algorithmic development and systematic benchmarking for the continuum robotics community.

</details>


### [109] [LIT-GS: LiDAR-Inertial-Thermal Gaussian Splatting for Illumination-Robust Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20424)
*Shikuan Shi,Chunran Zheng,Jiaming Xu,Tianyong Ye,Tao Yu,Yukang Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIT-GS 提出了一种结合激光雷达、惯性和热成像的高斯泼溅框架，利用激光雷达平面几何约束优化位姿与高斯渲染，在光照变化和纹理缺失场景下显著提升几何精度与渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LIV 高斯建图方法依赖 RGB 光度线索，在光照变化和纹理缺失场景下表现脆弱，亟需引入更鲁棒的几何约束。

Method: 1) 利用 LIV 视觉地图点作为置信度感知的跨模态锚点建立热-激光雷达关联；2) 在弱热监督下将加权激光雷达点到平面残差引入束调整以联合优化相机位姿和 3D 点；3) 引入激光雷达平面正则化的可微泼溅目标函数，约束渲染点对齐局部观测平面。

Result: 在私有序列和公开数据集上，LIT-GS 在几何精度和渲染质量上一致优于基于 LIV 的高斯泼溅方法，尤其在挑战性光照条件下表现突出。

Conclusion: 融合激光雷达平面几何约束是提升热-惯性-视觉高斯建图在退化场景下鲁棒性的有效途径。

Abstract: Gaussian Splatting has enabled real-time neural rendering, yet existing LiDAR-inertial-visual (LIV) Gaussian mapping pipelines remain fragile under illumination changes and texture-deficient scenes due to their reliance on RGB photometric cues. We present LIT-GS, a LiDAR-inertial-thermal Gaussian Splatting framework that injects LiDAR-derived plane geometry as an explicit constraint in both pose/structure refinement and Gaussian optimization. Specifically, we exploit LIV visual map points as confidence-aware cross-modal anchors to establish reliable thermal-LiDAR associations, and incorporate weighted LiDAR point-to-plane residuals into bundle adjustment to jointly refine camera poses and 3D points under weak thermal supervision. Building on the refined structure, we further introduce a LiDAR-plane-regularized differentiable splatting objective that constrains rendered 3D points to align with locally observed planes, mitigating surface thickening and structural drift in low-contrast thermal imagery. Experiments on proprietary sequences and public datasets demonstrate that LIT-GS consistently improves geometric accuracy and rendering quality over state-of-the-art LIV-based Gaussian Splatting baselines, particularly in challenging lighting conditions.

</details>


### [110] [TaCauchy: An Extensible FEM Framework for Vision-Based Tactile Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20426)
*Hengfei Zhao,Yifan Xie,Junhao Gong,Yue Sun,Kai Zhu,Weihua He,Shoujie Li,Haohuan Fu,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TaCauchy是一个基于有限元方法（FEM）的物理仿真框架，集成于Isaac Sim平台，通过UIPC求解器直接计算柯西应力张量，为视觉触觉传感器提供机械真值，实现高保真仿真，支持多种传感器快速集成，在60个并行环境中达到555 FPS吞吐量，物理验证SSIM超过0.93。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的触觉传感器仿真方法难以在GPU加速机器人平台上提供精确的机械应力场，缺乏基于第一性原理的力学真值，限制了强化学习方法的有效性。

Method: 基于统一增量势接触（UIPC）求解器，从超弹性本构方程直接计算柯西应力张量，并将其投影到接触表面获取牵引力和压力分布；采用几何感知自适应细化的自动网格生成；设计模块化传感器接口支持多种触觉传感器快速集成。

Result: 单环境33.40 FPS，60个并行环境聚合吞吐量555 FPS，应力提取开销低于1ms；在1.2556N至4.7332N力范围内，模拟与真实触觉响应之间SSIM超过0.93。

Conclusion: TaCauchy框架能够提供准确、基于物理的力监督信号，适用于下游机器人操作任务，验证了其作为物理真值来源的可靠性。

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensors require high-fidelity simulation for reinforcement learning, yet existing approaches struggle to provide accurate mechanical stress fields within GPU-accelerated robotics platforms. We present TaCauchy, an extensible Finite Element Method (FEM) framework that integrates rigorous physics-based force computation into Isaac Sim. Built on the Unified Incremental Potential Contact (UIPC) solver, TaCauchy directly computes Cauchy stress tensors from hyperelastic constitutive laws and projects them onto contact surfaces to obtain traction forces and pressure distributions, providing mechanical ground truth from first principles rather than empirical estimation. Our framework features automatic mesh generation with geometry-aware adaptive refinement and a modular sensor interface enabling rapid integration of diverse sensors (GelSight Mini, DIGIT, 9DTact) with minimal configuration. Performance benchmarks demonstrate 33.40 FPS for single environments and 555 FPS aggregate throughput across 60 parallel environments, with stress extraction overhead under 1 ms. Physical validation experiments show strong agreement between simulated and real tactile responses across force ranges from 1.2556 N to 4.7332 N, achieving SSIM above 0.93, confirming the framework's capability to provide accurate, physically-grounded force supervision for downstream robotic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [111] [ARC: Adaptive Robust Joint State and Covariance Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20428)
*Alexandre Hadji-Thomas,Andrew Stirling,James R. Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的块坐标下降框架，结合自适应鲁棒损失、迭代重加权最小二乘状态更新和最小加权协方差行列式估计，实现了在异常值存在下同时估计状态和协方差的自调优联合估计器，无需手动参数调节，在仿真和真实UWB定位实验中表现优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器测量常受异常值和非高斯噪声污染，导致经典状态估计器产生有偏且不可靠的估计。现有鲁棒估计器虽能抑制异常值，但不进行测量协方差估计；联合状态-协方差估计器则假设高斯残差和固定损失形状参数。将这两种能力整合到一个框架中，有望在存在异常值的情况下同时估计状态和协方差。

Method: 提出统一的块坐标下降（Block-Coordinate Descent）框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 范数感知的自适应鲁棒损失函数；2) 迭代重加权最小二乘（IRLS）状态更新；3) 最小加权协方差行列式（Minimum Weighted Covariance Determinant）协方差估计器。三者协同工作，实现自调优的联合状态与协方差估计。

Result: 在蒙特卡洛仿真和真实超宽带（UWB）定位实验（部署于杂乱的非视距环境）中，所提估计器一致地恢复了真实的内点测量协方差，并在状态估计精度上达到或超越所有基线方法，且无需任何手动参数调节。

Conclusion: 该统一框架成功将鲁棒估计与协方差估计能力集成，实现了无需人工调参的自动联合估计，在异常值场景下具有良好的实用性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Sensor measurements are frequently corrupted by outliers and non-Gaussian noise. These imperfections in the sensor data can cause classical state estimators to generate biased and unreliable state and uncertainty estimates. Robust estimators reject or downweight outliers but do not perform measurement covariance estimation, whereas joint state and covariance estimators assume Gaussian residuals and fixed loss shape parameters. Integrating these two capabilities into a single framework is an opportunity to simultaneously estimate both state and covariance in the presence of outliers. This paper proposes a unified Block-Coordinate Descent framework that combines a norm-aware adaptive robust loss, an Iteratively Reweighted Least-Squares state update, and a Minimum Weighted Covariance Determinant covariance estimator, yielding a self-tuning joint state and covariance estimator. The framework is evaluated in a Monte-Carlo simulation and on real-world ultra-wideband localization experiments in cluttered non-line-of-sight environments. Results show that the proposed estimator consistently recovers the true inlier measurement covariance and matches or exceeds the state estimation accuracy of all baselines, without requiring any manual parameter tuning.

</details>


### [112] [Slow Brain, Fast Planner: Latency-Resilient VLM-Augmented Urban Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20458)
*Zhenghao "Mark'' Peng,Honglin He,Quanyi Li,Yukai Ma,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM-Planner接口，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）从基于锚点的规划器生成的轨迹候选集中选择最优轨迹，并通过几何相似性+指数衰减的融合层解决VLM延迟（1-3秒）无法直接驱动实时控制（5-20Hz）的问题。在约2000个挑战性场景中，VLM选择将ADE降低30%，在延迟达5秒时仍保持80%以上成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的侧边导航规划器虽能实时生成多样轨迹，但其评分函数在挑战性场景中常无法选出最佳轨迹，导致机器人开上草坪、冲向行人或反向行驶。作者将这一现象定义为"轨迹评分差距"——规划器首选与候选集中最优轨迹之间存在显著差距，根源在于规划器缺乏高层场景理解能力。

Method: 提出VLM-Planner接口：1）VLM从规划器候选集中选择最佳轨迹索引；2）设计训练无关的延迟鲁棒轨迹级融合层，通过几何相似性+指数衰减将过时的VLM选择转化为实时规划器评分。该融合层解决了VLM推理延迟（1-3秒）与机器人控制频率（5-20Hz）不匹配的问题。

Result: 在约2000个真实挑战性场景（路口、行人相遇等）中，VLM选择相比规划器自身最佳选择实现30%的ADE（平均位移误差）降低；在常规场景中规划器仍具竞争力。仿真实验中，分数融合层在延迟高达5秒时仍保持>80%的成功率。在真实校园侧边导航机器人上验证了系统有效性。

Conclusion: VLM-Planner接口通过结合VLM的高层语义理解能力与规划器的实时控制能力，有效解决了轨迹评分差距问题，且无需替换现有规划器或进行端到端训练。延迟鲁棒的融合层设计使其适用于实际部署中的网络延迟变化场景。

Abstract: Learning-based planners for sidewalk navigation can generate diverse candidate trajectories in real time, yet their scoring functions often fail to select the best trajectory in challenging situations, outputting trajectories that make the mobile robot drive onto grass, toward pedestrians, or in the wrong direction, even when better candidates exist in the same set. We call this the trajectory scoring gap: in real-world sidewalk navigation, the gap between an anchor-based planner's top choice and the best possible candidate is substantial, likely due to limited high-level scene understanding capability of the planner. Rather than replacing the planner with an end-to-end Vision-Language-Action model, we propose a VLM-Planner interface that uses a VLM to select a candidate index from the planner's proposal set and then fuse it with the planner's initial output. However, VLMs take 1--3s per query and so cannot directly drive a 5--20Hz control loop. We contribute a training-free, latency-resilient trajectory-level fusion layer that turns a stale VLM selection into real-time planner scoring via geometric similarity with exponential decay. On $\sim$2,000 challenging real-world scenarios (e.g., junctions, pedestrian encounters), VLM selection achieves 30% ADE reduction versus the planner's best selection, while the planner remains competitive in routine situations. In simulation, Score Fusion maintains >80% success rate with delays up to 5s. We demonstrate the full system on a mobile robot navigating challenging campus sidewalks with varied network latency.

</details>


### [113] [GroundControl: Anticipating Navigation Failures in Vision-Language Agents via Trajectory-Consistent Uncertainty Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20479)
*Nastaran Darabi,Divake Kumar,Sina Tayebati,Devashri Naik,Amit Ranjan Trivedi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GroundControl，一种基于轨迹一致性的不确定性估计方法，通过卡尔曼滤波建模距离-目标动态的统计偏差来预测视觉语言导航代理的故障（如振荡、停滞、低效绕路），并在新提出的选择性风险-覆盖导航评估协议下，在五个EB-Navigation数据集上显著优于熵、共形和启发式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言导航代理虽然在平均成功率上表现良好，但常出现可预测的轨迹级故障（如振荡、停滞、低效绕路）。现有基于瞬时动作熵的不确定性信号无法有效捕捉这些随时间累积的故障动态，因此需要一种能够在执行过程中预测故障的信号。

Method: GroundControl建模距离-目标动态的统计偏差：使用恒定速度卡尔曼滤波器追踪距离演化，结合归一化创新统计量与互补轨迹特征（进度、单调性、路径效率、振荡行为），生成反映几何和时间不一致性的不确定性分数。

Result: 在五个EB-Navigation数据集（N=300）上，基于成功的选择性风险评估中，GPT-4o模型的加权平均E-AURC_SR=0.0024，接近oracle性能；在SPL评估下，GroundControl在所有模型和导航分割上均取得最低AURC和E-AURC。

Conclusion: 对目标导向动态的偏差建模为预测视觉语言导航代理失败提供了可解释且鲁棒的信号，优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Vision-language navigation agents achieve competitive average success on benchmark tasks, yet failures often arise through predictable trajectory-level breakdowns such as oscillation, stagnation, or inefficient detours. Reliable deployment, therefore, requires uncertainty signals that anticipate emerging failure dynamics during execution rather than reflect only instantaneous action entropy. We introduce \emph{GroundControl}, a trajectory-consistent uncertainty estimator defined as statistical deviation from nominal goal-directed distance-to-goal dynamics aggregated over an episode. GroundControl models distance evolution using a constant-velocity Kalman filter and combines normalized innovation statistics with complementary trajectory features capturing progress, monotonicity, path efficiency, and oscillatory behavior. The resulting uncertainty score reflects geometric and temporal inconsistency in navigation behavior rather than local prediction dispersion. To evaluate uncertainty quality independently of task success, we formalize \emph{Selective Risk--Coverage Navigation (SRCN)}, a protocol that measures how effectively an uncertainty score ranks episodes by failure or inefficiency using risk--coverage curves and AURC / E-AURC summaries. Across five EB-Navigation splits ($N=300$ episodes), trajectory-consistent uncertainty achieves near-oracle ordering under success-based selective risk, with weighted-average $\mathrm{E\text{-}AURC}_{\mathrm{SR}}=0.0024$ for the GPT-4o model, substantially outperforming entropy-, conformal-, and heuristic baselines. Under SPL-based selective evaluation, GroundControl consistently achieves the lowest AURC and E-AURC across models and navigation splits. These results show that modeling deviation from goal-directed dynamics provides an interpretable and robust signal for anticipating navigation failures in vision-language agents.

</details>


### [114] [Fast Human Attention Prediction for Fixation-guided Active Perception in Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20491)
*Fatma Youssef Mohammed,Grzegorz Malczyk,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GazeLNN是一种基于液态神经网络和MobileNetV3的轻量级扫描路径预测模型，仅需0.61 GFLOPs，计算成本降低99.40%，推理速度提升6倍，在MIT Low Resolution数据集上达到SOTA性能（ScanMatch得分0.47），并成功集成到主动摄像头-机器人控制策略中，实现真实世界无人机部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉通过结构化扫描路径高效处理场景，但现有预测模型计算成本高昂，阻碍了该能力在机器人自主系统中的实际应用。作者旨在开发一种计算轻量的扫描路径预测模型，以解决机器人自主系统中人类注意力建模的高计算成本问题。

Method: 提出GazeLNN架构：以液态神经网络（LNN）作为循环引擎，MobileNetV3用于特征提取；采用自回归方式，根据当前视觉刺激和注视历史预测序列化的注视热力图。随后将GazeLNN集成到基于强化学习训练的主动摄像头-机器人控制策略中。

Result: 在MIT Low Resolution数据集上达到0.47 ScanMatch得分，超越现有循环基线模型；计算成本降低99.40%，推理速度提升6倍（仅需0.61 GFLOPs）；在真实世界的无人机平台上成功完成自主导航验证。

Conclusion: GazeLNN以极低计算成本实现了SOTA扫描路径预测性能，验证了高效的类人注意力建模在机器人自主系统中的实用价值，为注意力引导的机器人感知提供了可行的轻量级解决方案。

Abstract: Human visual attention relies on structured scanpaths to efficiently process scenes, yet instilling this behavior into robot autonomy is in its infancy and hindered by the high,computational costs of existing predictive models. To address this, we introduce GazeLNN, a computationally lightweight,scanpath prediction model that leverages Liquid Neural Networks as its recurrent engine and employs MobileNetV3 for feature extraction. Operating auto-regressively, the architecture predicts sequential fixation heatmaps conditioned on the current visual stimulus and fixation history. Despite requiring only 0.61 GFLOPs, GazeLNN achieves state-of-the-art performance on the MIT Low Resolution dataset achieving 0.47 ScanMatch score. It outperforms existing recurrent baselines across diverse evaluation metrics, while reducing computational costs by 99.40% and accelerating inference by up to six times. To investigate the role of human attention modeling in robot autonomy and demonstrate the practical utility of this highly efficient architecture, we integrate GazeLNN into an active camera-robot control policy trained via Reinforcement Learning. This integration enables human-fixation-guided perception during autonomous navigation, validated through successful real-world deployments on an aerial robot.

</details>


### [115] [Increasing Resilience of Continuum Robots via Motion Planning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20495)
*Oxana Shamilyan,Ievgen Kabin,Zoya Dyka,Oleksandr Sudakov,Peter Langendoerfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文实验研究了弹性连续机器人的运动规划，重点是多准则决策及其对路径规划算法的影响。通过将遗传算法和A*算法与层次分析法（AHP）结合，考虑距离、电机损伤、机械臂损伤和精度四个准则来评估路径质量。实验在两种模拟环境中进行，结果表明遗传算法的执行时间不受环境基数影响，能生成更多样化的路径，从而提升机器人弹性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升连续机器人的弹性，延长其维护周期，需要引入多准则决策来优化路径规划，而不仅仅是单一的距离优化。

Method: 将遗传算法和A*算法与层次分析法（AHP）结合，AHP考虑距离、电机损伤、机械臂损伤和精度四个准则，在两种模拟环境（单/多路径点和仅多路径点）中进行实验。

Result: 遗传算法的执行时间不受环境基数影响；相比A*算法，遗传算法能生成更多样化的路径，从而提升机器人弹性。

Conclusion: 遗传算法在弹性连续机器人的路径规划中优于A*算法，其环境无关的执行时间和路径多样性使其更适合多准则决策场景。

Abstract: This paper presents an experimental study of motion planning for resilient continuum robots. In this study we mainly focused on multi-criteria decision-making, its application for path-planning algorithms, impact on the generated path and execution time. To do this, we used two well-known algorithms for path planning, namely Genetic algorithm and A star algorithm, and modified them by adding the Analytical Hierarchy Process algorithm to evaluate the quality of the paths generated. In our experiment the Analytical Hierarchy Process considers four different criteria, i.e. distance, motors damage, mechanical damage of the robot's arm and accuracy, each considered to contribute to the resilience of a continuum robot. The use of different criteria is necessary to increase the time to maintenance operations of the continuum robot. We conducted the experiments using two different simulated environments of the robot. Although we significantly simplified the robot's model and its environment, we still implemented some of the features of the environment based on the real robot prototype. In particular, one of the environments has single- as well as multi-path points, and other consists of the multi-path points only. The results show that, in contrast to A star, the performance time of Genetic algorithm does not depend on the environment's cardinality. It generates more diverse paths, which increases the robot's resilience.

</details>


### [116] [Generating Robot Hands from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20549)
*Sha Yi,Nicklas Hansen,Xueqian Bai,Carmelo Sferrazza,Michael T. Tolley,Xiaolong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个数据驱动的机器人手部设计框架，利用超过400万帧人类手指运动数据，通过逆运动学匹配指尖位置来优化树形结构的机器人手部设计，生成了6自由度通用手和低自由度任务专用手，并利用强化学习加速设计搜索，实际实验中实现了优于商用机器人的精确遥操作指尖追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习在控制方面进展迅速，但物理身体的设计仍然困难，因为同时搜索设计与控制是一个巨大的组合优化问题。当前的机器人手部设计往往需要为每个候选设计学习复杂的控制器，效率低下。

Method: (1) 利用超过400万帧人类日常操作的指尖运动数据；(2) 采用逆运动学匹配指尖位置的简单控制策略，替代学习复杂控制器；(3) 优化树形结构的机器人手部设计以复现目标运动；(4) 训练强化学习智能体提出好的手部设计和关节角度，将搜索时间从数小时缩短至数分钟；(5) 直接以一体式铰链结构进行3D打印制造。

Result: 6自由度通用手实现了高精度遥操作指尖追踪，性能优于现有商用机器人手；3自由度专用手以更低的机械复杂度复现了结构化的人类和合成轨迹；强化学习将设计搜索时间从小时级降至分钟级。

Conclusion: 大规模人类运动数据不仅可用于训练机器人控制器，还可作为优化和生成机器人物理形态的参考依据，证明了数据驱动的方法可以从运动数据直接生成有效的机器人硬件设计。

Abstract: Robot learning has advanced rapidly in learning control, but learning the physical body of a robot remains much more difficult because jointly searching over design and control creates a very large combinatorial problem. Here, we present a data-driven framework for generating robot hands from human demonstrations. Instead of learning a complex controller together with each candidate design, we generate robot hand designs using the same simple control policy used after fabrication: matching fingertip positions through inverse kinematics. Using more than 4 million frames of human fingertip motion from everyday manipulation, our algorithm optimizes tree-structured robot hands to reproduce desired target motions. The framework produced both a 6-degree-of-freedom (DoF) general-purpose hand and lower-DoF task-specific hands with spatial four-bar mimic joints. To accelerate the search over designs, we trained a reinforcement-learning (RL) actor to propose good hand designs and joint angles, reducing search time from hours to minutes. We fabricated the mechanisms directly as one-piece articulated structures with print-in-place joints. In real-world experiments, the 6-DoF hand achieved highly accurate teleoperated fingertip tracking better than available commercial robot hands, whereas the specialized 3-DoF hands reproduced structured human and synthetic trajectories with reduced mechanical complexity. These results showed that large-scale human motion data can be used not only to train robot controllers but also as a reference for optimizing and generating the physical embodiment of robots.

</details>


### [117] [MemoryWAM: Efficient World Action Modeling with Persistent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20562)
*Sizhe Yang,Juncheng Mu,Tianming Wei,Chenhao Lu,Xiaofan Li,Linning Xu,Zhengrong Xue,Zhecheng Yuan,Dahua Lin,Jiangmiao Pang,Huazhe Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MemoryWAM提出了一种混合记忆设计的世界动作模型，结合近期帧、事件边界锚点帧和压缩要点标记，通过定制化注意力机制实现长序列任务的高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAMs）在非马尔可夫环境中面临根本性权衡：高效推理方法仅依赖有限窗口的近期观测，难以处理长程依赖；而保留完整历史的方法则随着序列长度增长导致时间和空间成本急剧上升。

Method: 采用混合记忆设计，包含三种记忆组件：近期帧（详细短程上下文）、事件边界锚点帧（关键事件节点）和压缩要点标记（概括长程历史）；并设计定制化注意力机制，同时支持短程细节检索和长程压缩上下文检索。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界的长时域、依赖记忆的操控任务中，MemoryWAM优于强视觉-语言-动作（VLA）和WAM基线方法，同时保持有利的计算效率（更低的推理延迟和GPU内存占用）。

Conclusion: 高效的持久记忆机制能够在不牺牲计算效率的前提下，显著提升世界动作模型在非马尔可夫环境中的长程依赖建模能力，为机器人操控中的记忆与动力学建模提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Robust robotic manipulation in the real world requires not only an understanding of the current observation, but also memory and dynamics modeling. World action models (WAMs) possess these capabilities by jointly modeling visual foresight and actions conditioned on both current and historical observations, making them a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation. However, existing WAMs face a fundamental trade-off: methods with efficient inference typically condition only on a bounded window of recent observations and therefore struggle in non-Markovian environments, whereas methods that preserve long histories incur time and space costs that grow substantially with sequence length. To address this challenge, we introduce MemoryWAM, a world action model with efficient persistent memory. MemoryWAM uses a hybrid memory design that combines recent frames, event-boundary anchor frames, and compact gist tokens that summarize long-range history. A tailored attention mechanism enables retrieval of both detailed short-term context and compressed long-term context, supporting memory-dependent decision-making with reduced inference latency and GPU memory usage. Across long-horizon, memory-dependent manipulation tasks in both simulation and the real world, MemoryWAM outperforms strong vision-language-action (VLA) and WAM baselines while maintaining favorable computational efficiency.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Neural Architectures as Functional Priors in Physics-Informed Control Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19368)
*Sonia Rubio Herranz,Fernando Carlos López Hernández,Antonio López Montes*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了神经网络架构作为隐式函数先验在ODE控制问题中的作用，通过线性RLC电路和非线性Duffing系统的实验发现：即使在完全相同的问题设置下，不同架构（MLP vs Fourier-based KAN）会系统性地产生性质不同的控制策略，体现在频谱结构、平滑度、能量分布和相空间行为上。关键发现是"功能特化"现象——Fourier架构倾向于产生更丰富的振荡内容，而低频偏置架构产生更规则、更节能的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经网络架构本身在控制问题中扮演的隐式函数先验角色，而非关注复杂问题，旨在最简单、物理可解释的设置下研究架构依赖效应。

Method: 对比多层感知机（MLP）和基于Fourier的KAN类架构，通过经典最优控制公式和PINN方法分析线性RLC电路和非线性Duffing系统，在保持控制方程、损失泛函、初始/目标状态、训练参数和物理约束完全相同的情况下进行实验。

Result: 不同架构系统性地产生性质上不同的控制策略；显著差异出现在频谱结构、平滑度、能量分布和相空间行为上；观察到了功能特化现象。

Conclusion: Fourier架构倾向于产生更丰富的振荡控制轨迹，低频偏置架构产生更规则、更节能的控制；不同功能组件可能由不同神经架构更高效地处理，导致状态表征和控制生成之间的隐式特化。

Abstract: In this work we investigate the role of neural architectures as implicit functional priors in control problems governed by ordinary differential equations. Rather than focusing on highly complex problems, our objective is to investigate architecture-dependent effects in controlled dynamical systems within the simplest physically interpretable settings possible. In particular, we study a controlled linear RLC electrical circuit and a nonlinear Duffing-type dynamical system. Both systems are analyzed first through classical optimal-control formulations and later through PINN-based approaches. We compare different combinations of multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) and Fourier-based KAN-like architectures, and analyze their influence on the resulting controls. The numerical experiments suggest that different architectural choices systematically generate qualitatively distinct controls, even under identical governing equations, loss functionals, initial and target states, training parameters and physical constraints. Significant differences appear in the spectral structure, smoothness, energy distribution, and phase-space behavior of the learned solutions. A central observation of this work is the emergence of a functional specialization phenomenon when the neural architectures are allowed sufficient freedom to shape the structure of the learned controls. More specifically, in the systems considered here, Fourier-based architectures tend to produce trajectories with richer oscillatory content, whereas smoother low-frequency-biased architectures tend to generate more regular and energetically efficient controls. This suggests that different functional components of the control problem may be handled more efficiently by different neural architectures, leading to an implicit specialization between state representation and control generation.

</details>


### [119] [Moreau-Yosida-based Kohn-Sham Inversion for Periodic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19471)
*Vebjørn H. Bakkestuen,Michael F. Herbst,Vegard Falmår,Markus Penz,Andre Laestadius*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文在Moreau-Yosida正则化密度泛函理论框架下，研究了周期系统的密度-势能反演问题，通过理论分析和数值实验展示了该方法在Kohn-Sham和Gross-Pitaevskii方程中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在周期系统中，从密度反演外部势能（尤其是交换关联势）是密度泛函理论中的一个关键问题，但目前缺乏严格的数学框架和高效的数值方法。

Method: 在周期齐次Sobolev空间中建立框架，利用Moreau-Yosida正则化，通过近端映射及其算法实现，并结合非相互作用动能泛函在所选拓扑下的下半连续性证明，通过极限过程恢复Kohn-Sham交换关联势。

Result: 成功实现了周期系统中密度-势能的反演，数值实验验证了该方法对Kohn-Sham方程和Gross-Pitaevskii方程的有效性。

Conclusion: Moreau-Yosida正则化结合近端映射为周期系统的密度-势能反演提供了严格的数学基础和可行的数值方案。

Abstract: Density-potential inversion for periodic systems within Moreau-Yosida-regularised density-functional theory is investigated, both theoretically and numerically. We develop the framework in a periodic homogeneous Sobolev space and use it to recover the exchange-correlation potential of Kohn-Sham theory through a limiting procedure. A key analytical ingredient is the proof of lower semicontinuity of the non-interacting kinetic-energy functional in the chosen topology. The proximal mapping, together with its algorithmic evaluation, plays a central role in the resulting inversion scheme. Numerical experiments illustrate the performance and properties of the method for both the Kohn-Sham and Gross-Pitaevskii equations.

</details>


### [120] [Higher Accuracy Modular Data Assimilation for the Navier-Stokes Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19508)
*Troy Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将二阶向后差分时间离散（BDF2）与模块化两步微推数据同化相结合的方法，证明了其稳定性和误差估计，并通过数值实验表明该方法在保持与标准全耦合数据同化相当精度的同时，大幅降低了计算复杂度和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全耦合数据同化方法计算复杂度高、成本大，亟需一种在保持精度的同时降低计算负担的模块化数据同化方法。

Method: 将BDF2时间离散格式与模块化两步（预报-分析）微推数据同化结合；分析步采用隐式稳定性质的显式表达形式，降低复杂度。

Result: 推导了BDF2模块化同化算法的稳定性和误差估计；数值实验表明该方法与标准全耦合数据同化精度相当，但计算复杂度和成本显著降低。

Conclusion: 模块化数据同化方法在保持精度的前提下可大幅降低计算成本，是一种高效可行的替代方案。

Abstract: This paper develops an accurate and effective combination of second order backward differentiation time discretization (BDF2) with modular, 2-step nudging-based data assimilation \begin{align} \text{Forecast step: } \quad &\frac{3\widetilde{v}^{n+2}-4v^{n+1}+v^n}{2Δt}+\widetilde{v}^{n+2} \cdot \nabla \widetilde{v}^{n+2} - νΔ\widetilde{v}^{n+2} + \nabla q^{n+2}=f(x) \notag \\ &\nabla \cdot \widetilde{v}^{n+2} = 0 \notag \\ \text{Analysis step: } \quad &\frac{3v^{n+2}-3\widetilde{v}^{n+2}}{2Δt}-χI_H(u(t^{n+2})-v^{n+2})=0. \notag \end{align} If $I_H=I_H^2$, the analysis step can be made explicit, taking the form \begin{align} v^{n+2}=\widetilde{v}^{n+2}+\frac{2Δtχ}{3+2Δtχ}I_H(u^{n+2}-\widetilde{v}^{n+2}). \notag \end{align} This implies the analysis step has the stability property of an implicit step and lower complexity than an explicit analysis step. Stability and error estimates for the BDF2 scheme are presented along with their proofs. Numerical experiments are conducted to assess the performance of BDF2 modular assimilation algorithm. The results of the experiments support the conclusion that modular data assimilation has comparable accuracy to standard, fully coupled data assimilation while greatly reducing computational complexity and cost.

</details>


### [121] [Bregman-projected mirror methods for regularized stationary mean-field games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19611)
*Hussain Al Abdulaziz,Yuri Ashrafyan,Yeva Gevorgyan,Diogo Gomes*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对具有二次或超二次Hamiltonian增长和线性或超线性密度耦合的静态平均场博弈系统，本文提出了一种基于Bregman投影的镜像迭代方法，用于低阶正则化变分不等式求解，并证明了在可和步长条件下约束迭代强收敛到唯一解，数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为静态平均场博弈系统的低阶正则化问题设计一种适配混合Lebesgue-Sobolev指标的Bregman几何结构的迭代方法，并建立严格的收敛性理论，以克服标准方法在Banach空间设置下的数值求解困难。

Method: 将静态MFG系统的低阶γ-Laplacian正则化表述为L^β×W^{1,γ}上的变分不等式；引入与混合Lebesgue-Sobolev指数匹配的Bregman几何结构；设计带冻结算子评估的约束两步镜像方法；推导单步Bregman不等式并利用步长可和条件证明强收敛性。

Result: 推导了约束迭代的单步Bregman不等式；证明了在步长自然可和条件下，约束迭代强收敛到正则化变分不等式的唯一解；一维和二维数值实验验证了残差随网格细化而衰减，两步实现在离散化中表现出更优的实际性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的Bregman投影镜像迭代方法为求解低阶正则化MFG系统提供了可靠的数值框架，其收敛性理论完备且数值表现良好，为更复杂MFG模型的数值求解奠定了基础。

Abstract: We develop and analyze a Bregman-projected mirror iteration for low-order regularizations of stationary mean-field game (MFG) systems in their natural Banach space setting. For separable Hamiltonians of the form \(H(x,p,m)=H_0(x,p)-g(m)\), with quadratic or super-quadratic Hamiltonian growth and linear or super-linear density couplings, we formulate a low-order \(\barγ\)-Laplacian regularization of the stationary MFG system as a variational inequality on \(L^{\barβ}(\mathbb T^d)\times W^{1,\barγ}(\mathbb T^d)\). To approximate solutions of this regularized variational inequality, we introduce a Bregman geometry matched to the mixed Lebesgue--Sobolev exponents of the problem and analyze a constrained two-step mirror method with frozen operator evaluation. For the exact constrained iteration and each fixed regularization parameter \(\epsi>0\), we derive a one-step Bregman inequality and use it to prove that the constrained iteration converges strongly to the unique solution of the regularized variational inequality under natural summability conditions on the step sizes. Numerical experiments on one- and two-dimensional models, validated against exact test solutions, illustrate residual decay under mesh refinement and suggest improved practical performance of the two-step implementation in the tested discretizations.

</details>


### [122] [Second order explicit splitting scheme for fluid-poroelastic structure interaction problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19811)
*Yifan Wang,Jeonghun Lee,Suncica Canic*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对固定域上含时Stokes-Biot问题，提出并分析了一种全离散、二阶精度、显式分裂格式。该方法结合BDF2时间步进与Robin重构下的二阶Adams-Bashforth界面数据外推，实现了Stokes子问题和Biot子问题的独立并行求解。主要理论贡献包括在抛物型CFL条件下的稳定性证明以及基于投影框架的先验误差估计，数值实验验证了二阶时间收敛和最优阶空间收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流体与多孔弹性结构相互作用的数值模拟中，显式处理Stokes-Biot界面耦合条件可能导致数值不稳定，而现有的分区方法在高效性和可证明精度方面仍面临挑战。为克服这一困难，需要开发兼具高效率与严格稳定性保证的二阶显式耦合格式。

Method: 采用BDF2时间离散结合二阶Adams-Bashforth界面数据外推，并通过Robin重构处理界面耦合条件，形成Stokes和Biot子问题独立并行的分区算法。稳定性分析借助BDF2能量等式、外推界面项的尖锐分解及离散迹估计；误差分析则通过Fortin投影（流体变量）和Ritz型投影（多孔弹性变量）的投影框架推导先验误差估计。

Result: 在抛物型CFL条件下证明了封闭稳定性界；误差分析表明总误差（流体速度、结构速度、孔隙压力、弹性位移）在能量范数下由C×（网格大小的k次方+时间步长的平方）控制，其中k=1,2,3。数值实验验证了二阶时间收敛和最优阶空间收敛，并在移动域Navier-Stokes算例中展示了方法的适用性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的显式分裂格式在保证严格稳定性的同时实现了二阶精度，是首个针对Stokes-Biot问题具有严格稳定性和误差分析的二阶显式耦合方案，且可推广至更复杂的移动域问题。

Abstract: Efficient and provably accurate partitioned methods for fluid-poroelastic structure interaction remain challenging because explicit treatment of the Stokes-Biot interface coupling condition can compromise stability. In this work, we develop and analyze a fully discrete, second-order, explicit splitting scheme for the time-dependent Stokes-Biot problem on fixed domains. The method combines BDF2 time stepping with second-order Adams-Bashforth extrapolation of interface data through a Robin reformulation, yielding a partitioned algorithm in which the Stokes and Biot subproblems are solved independently and in parallel at each time step.
  The main analytical contribution is a rigorous stability and error analysis for this second-order explicit coupling strategy. Using BDF2 energy identities, a sharp decomposition of the extrapolated interface terms, and discrete trace estimates, we prove a closed stability bound under a parabolic CFL condition. We then derive an a priori error estimate through a projection-based framework using a Fortin projection for the fluid variables and Ritz-type projections for the poroelastic variables. The analysis identifies consistency defects from BDF2 time discretization, Adams-Bashforth interface extrapolation, and the projected kinematic relation. It shows that the total errors in fluid velocity, structure velocity, pore pressure, and elastic displacement are bounded by C times the sum of the kth power of the mesh size and the square of the time step, for k from 1 to 3, in bulk energy norms.
  Numerical experiments with manufactured solutions confirm second-order temporal convergence and optimal-order spatial convergence. We also include a moving-domain example with Navier-Stokes fluid flow, demonstrating applicability beyond the fixed-domain Stokes-Biot setting analyzed.

</details>


### [123] [On a class of modified Cayley--Magnus methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19614)
*Sergio Blanes,Fernando Casas,Arieh Iserles*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新的数值积分器，用于求解系数矩阵稀疏且在二次矩阵李群中演化的非自治线性常微分方程。新方法避免计算矩阵指数作用于向量，转而求解一系列稀疏系数线性系统，适用于无界算子问题，并构造了四阶和六阶优化格式，数值实验表明优于现有李群积分器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有李群积分器需要计算矩阵指数作用于向量，计算成本高，且不适用于无界算子问题（易产生无界解），因此需要一种更高效、稳定的数值积分方法。

Method: 提出一类新数值积分器，通过求解一系列稀疏系数线性系统替代矩阵指数计算，针对非自治线性ODE设计，系数矩阵稀疏且在二次矩阵李群中演化，构造了四阶和六阶优化格式。

Result: 构造了四阶和六阶优化方案，数值示例表明新方法相比现有李群积分器具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 新方法避免了矩阵指数计算，通过求解稀疏线性系统实现更高效的时间积分，天然产生有界解，适合无界算子问题，且高阶优化格式性能优越。

Abstract: We introduce a new class of numerical integrators for the time integration of non-autonomous linear ordinary differential equations whose coefficient matrix is sparse and evolves within a quadratic matrix Lie group. In contrast to standard Lie group integrators, the proposed methods avoid the evaluation of matrix exponentials acting on vectors and instead rely on solving a sequence of linear systems with sparse coefficient matrices. Moreover, they are well suited for problems arising from unbounded operators, as they inherently produce bounded solutions. We construct optimised schemes of orders four and six and assess their performance on a representative numerical example, demonstrating clear advantages over existing Lie-group integrators.

</details>


### [124] [Explicit Fourier Integrator for the Periodic dNLS via Gauge Transformation: Low-Regularity Estimates in Discrete Bourgain Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19648)
*Lun Ji,Hang Li,Alexander Ostermann,Gangfan Zhong*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 针对一维环面（周期边界）上的导数非线性薛定谔方程，本文研究了一种滤波显式傅里叶积分器。通过周期规范变换和频率截断模型，结合滤波指数-欧拉离散化，并发展离散Bourgain空间框架处理周期情形下导数三次非线性的困难。对于初值 $u_0 \in H^s(\mathbb{T})$ ($1/2 < s \le 5/2$)，证明了数值误差在 $H^{1/2}(\mathbb{T})$ 中为 $\mathcal{O}(τ^{s/2-1/4})$ 阶，数值实验验证了收敛性预测和滤波格式对粗糙解的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究周期边界下导数非线性薛定谔方程的高效数值方法，解决周期情形中导数三次非线性带来的局部光滑性缺失和共振相互作用增强的困难，为粗糙初值提供稳定的数值格式。

Method: 采用周期规范变换对方程进行预处理，引入频率截断模型，设计滤波指数-欧拉离散化格式，并建立适应于规范变换后方程的离散Bourgain空间框架进行误差分析。

Result: 对于 $H^s(\mathbb{T})$ 初值 ($1/2 < s \le 5/2$)，数值误差在 $H^{1/2}(\mathbb{T})$ 范数下达到 $\mathcal{O}(τ^{s/2-1/4})$ 阶收敛率。数值实验验证了理论预测的收敛行为，并展示了滤波格式处理粗糙解的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的滤波显式傅里叶积分器能有效求解周期导数非线性薛定谔方程，收敛阶依赖于初值正则性，该格式对粗糙解具有良好鲁棒性。

Abstract: The derivative nonlinear Schrödinger equation is a fundamental model for the propagation of nonlinear dispersive waves in, for example, plasma physics and nonlinear optics. In this work, we consider this model on the one-dimensional torus and study a filtered explicit Fourier integrator for the corresponding periodic problem. After applying a periodic gauge transformation, we consider a frequency-truncated model and its filtered exponential-Euler discretization. The main difficulty comes from the derivative cubic nonlinearity in the periodic setting, since local smoothing is unavailable and resonant interactions are stronger than in the non-periodic case. To address this issue, we develop a discrete Bourgain-space framework adapted to the gauge-transformed equation. For initial data $u_0 \in H^s(\mathbb{T})$ with $1/2 < s \le 5/2$, we prove that the numerical error is of order $\mathcal{O}(τ^{s/2-1/4})$ in $H^{1/2}(\mathbb{T})$, where $τ$ denotes the employed time step size. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted convergence behavior and demonstrate the effectiveness of the filtered scheme for rough solutions.

</details>


### [125] [A Gradient Recovery Method for Electron Magnetohydrodynamics with Fractional Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19716)
*Hailong Guo,Ruimeng Hu,Qirui Peng,Xu Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出并分析了一种用于2.5D电子磁流体动力学系统的保结构数值方法，采用分量形式结合梯度恢复算子，建立半隐式能量稳定性，利用显式Hall积分因子高效计算，通过谱Hodge投影约束面内散度，数值实验验证了二阶空间收敛性和稳定的Hall驱动动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对2.5D电子磁流体动力学系统（含分数阶耗散），需要一种保结构、能量稳定且高效的数值方法，以准确捕捉Hall驱动动力学行为。

Method: 采用磁场分量形式，结合梯度恢复算子；半隐式保结构格式保证离散能量稳定性；显式Hall积分因子实现高效周期性网格计算；分数阶耗散在傅里叶空间精确处理；谱Hodge投影强制满足面内散度约束。

Result: 数值实验展示了二阶空间收敛性，并在多个基准测试中验证了稳定的Hall驱动动力学。

Conclusion: 所提出的保结构数值方法能有效求解2.5D电子MHD系统，兼具能量稳定性、计算效率和高精度，适用于Hall驱动等离子体动力学模拟。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a structure-preserving numerical method for the $2\tfrac{1}{2}$-dimensional (2.5D) electron magnetohydrodynamics system with fractional dissipation on the periodic torus. The method works directly with the magnetic field components and combines this component formulation with the gradient recovery operator of [T. Chu, H. Guo, and Z. Zhang, SIAM J. Numer. Anal., 63 (2025), pp. 23--53]. We establish discrete energy stability for a semi-implicit structure-preserving formulation and use an explicit-Hall integrating-factor implementation for efficient computation on periodic grids. The fractional dissipation is treated exactly in Fourier space, and the in-plane divergence constraint is enforced by a spectral Hodge projection. Numerical experiments demonstrate second-order spatial convergence and stable Hall-driven dynamics across several benchmark tests.

</details>


### [126] [Well-balanced second-order approximation of the compressible atmospheric Euler equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19764)
*Crystal Farris,Matthias Maier,Eric J. Tovar*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对含重力可压缩大气欧拉方程，提出了一种二阶近似方法，该方法具有保不变域和静力平衡状态下的平衡保持特性。通过基于密度静力重构的辅助离散状态和数值状态的仿射平移，实现了方法的平衡保持和保不变域性质，并用解析解和基准问题验证了方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气流动模拟中，需要数值方法同时满足保不变域（确保物理量在合理范围内）和平衡保持（精确维持静力平衡稳态）两个关键性质，而现有方法难以兼顾。

Method: 基于密度的静力重构构造离散辅助状态，结合数值状态的仿射平移，提供维持平衡保持和保不变域性质所需的局部界，构建二阶近似格式。

Result: 通过解析解、平衡保持测试以及典型大气流动基准问题的数值验证，证明了方法的有效性和准确性。

Conclusion: 所提二阶近似方法成功兼顾了保不变域和平衡保持两个关键性质，适用于大气流动问题的数值模拟。

Abstract: We introduce a second-order approximation to the compressible atmospheric Euler equations with gravity that is invariant domain preserving and well-balanced with respect to rest states. The approximation is built upon discrete auxiliary states derived from a hydrostatic reconstruction of the density. These auxiliary states, together with an affine shift of the numerical state, provide local bounds needed for maintaining well-balancing and invariant domain preserving properties of the method. The numerical method is then verified and validated with analytic solutions, well-balancing tests, and typical benchmark problems for atmospheric flows.

</details>


### [127] [Invariant measures of the stochastic theta method for stochastic differential equations with super-linearly growing coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19886)
*Xiaotong Li,Wei Liu,Wenjie Xiao*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对漂移和扩散系数均可能超线性增长的随机微分方程（SDEs），提出使用随机theta方法逼近其不变测度，证明了数值不变测度的存在唯一性及其对精确不变测度的收敛性，并提供了数值模拟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将已有文献中关于随机theta方法逼近SDE不变测度的结果从扩散系数线性增长情形推广到超线性增长情形；同时将后向Euler-Maruyama方法的相关结果推广到更一般的随机theta方法。

Method: 采用随机theta方法离散原SDE，首先证明数值解生成的不变测度的存在唯一性，然后证明该数值不变测度收敛到原SDE的精确不变测度，最后通过数值模拟验证理论结果。

Result: 成功证明了在漂移和扩散系数均超线性增长条件下，随机theta方法生成的数值不变测度存在唯一且收敛于精确不变测度。

Conclusion: 该工作将已有结果推广到了更一般的情形（扩散系数超线性增长以及从后向EM到随机theta方法），拓展了随机theta方法在不变测度逼近问题上的适用范围。

Abstract: The stochastic theta method is proposed to approximate invariant measures of stochastic differential equations (SDEs), both of whose drift and diffusion coefficients may grow super-linearly. For the numerical solution generated by the stochastic theta method, we show the existence and uniqueness of the numerical invariant measure first. Then, we prove that the numerical invariant measure is convergent to the exact invariant measure of the underlying SDE. We also provide some numerical simulations to illustrate our theoretical results. This work could be regarded as an extension of the results in [Y. Jiang et al, Numer. Algorithms 83(4)(2020), pp. 1531-1553] to the case of super-linearly growing diffusion coefficient. As the backward Euler-Maruyama (EM) method is a special case of the stochastic theta method, the results derived in this work could also be regarded as a generalization of the results for the backward EM method in [W. Liu et al. Appl. Numer. Math. 184(2023), pp. 137-150] to the stochastic theta method.

</details>


### [128] [A fast direct solver based neural network for solving PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19895)
*Jashwanth Reddy Kadaru,Vaishnavi Gujjula*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于HODLR矩阵快速直接求解器的神经网络架构，用于学习HODLR矩阵的逆运算，并通过将部分线性层替换为深度子网络扩展为非线性PDE解算子。实验涵盖Fredholm积分方程、非线性薛定谔方程、Burgers方程和稳态Darcy流方程，验证了其泛化能力、推理速度和与经典数值求解器及现有神经算子网络的对比优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模N体问题产生的矩阵可用层级矩阵高效表示，其中HODLR矩阵是一类重要子类。本文旨在利用神经网络学习HODLR矩阵的逆运算，并进一步扩展为学习PDE非线性解算子，以提升求解效率。

Method: 基于Ambikasaran和Darve (2013)提出的HODLR矩阵快速直接求解器，设计神经网络学习其逆运算；通过将部分线性层替换为深度子网络，将架构扩展为非线性解算子学习网络。

Result: 在Fredholm积分方程、非线性薛定谔方程、Burgers方程和稳态Darcy流方程上验证了性能；进行了参数泛化研究；比较了推理时间与经典数值求解器运行时间；与现有神经算子学习网络进行了对比。

Conclusion: 所提出的架构能有效学习HODLR矩阵逆运算及PDE非线性解算子，在求解效率和泛化能力上具有优势。

Abstract: The matrices arising from large scale $N$-body problems can be efficiently represented using hierarchical matrices, whose key idea is that the admissible off-diagonal sub-matrices can be well approximated by low-rank matrices across a hierarchy of matrix partitions. HODLR (Hierarchical Off-Diagonal Low-Rank) matrices are a subclass of hierarchical matrices in which all off-diagonal submatrices at every level of a recursive binary partition are low-rank. In this article, we present a neural network that learns the inverse operation of HODLR matrices based on the fast direct solver for HODLR matrices developed by Ambikasaran and Darve (2013). We further extend the architecture to learn nonlinear solution operators associated with PDEs by replacing some of the linear layers with deep sub-networks. We demonstrate the performance of the proposed architecture by performing a comprehensive set of experiments that include (i) solving a linear problem such as the Fredholm integral equation of the second kind, (ii) solving PDEs such as the nonlinear Schrödinger equation, Burgers' equation, and the steady-state Darcy's flow equation, (iii) generalization study across varying parameter values, (iv) comparing the inference time of the proposed network with the run time of a classical numerical solver, and (v) comparing the proposed network with some of the existing neural operator learning networks.

</details>


### [129] [Structure-Oriented Randomized Neural Networks for Poisson-Nernst-Planck and Poisson-Nernst-Planck-Navier-Stokes Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19912)
*Yunlong Li,Fei Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向PNP和PNP-NS系统的结构导向随机神经网络框架SO-RaNN，通过解耦线性化子问题的迭代求解、点截断正性保持、离散质量缩放因子插值、SAV后处理修正以及速度场的结构保持随机神经网络(SP-RaNN)等技术，实现了值级正性、质量守恒、能量耗散和无散度约束的精确保持，并给出了相应的理论收敛性分析和数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PNP和PNP-NS系统在离子输运、电动力学等应用中至关重要，但其强耦合非线性和多物理场约束特性使得传统数值方法面临巨大挑战。现有方法难以同时保证浓度正性、质量守恒、能量耗散和无散度等关键物理约束，亟需一种能够精确保持系统结构特性的高效数值框架。

Method: 提出SO-RaNN框架：1) 在时空框架下用随机神经网络迭代求解解耦线性化子问题；2) 浓度变量采用点截断强制值级正性，在选定修正时刻计算离散质量缩放因子并时间插值以实现精确质量匹配；3) 采用SAV型后处理修正引入辅助离散耗散机制，保证SAV辅助变量单调递减；4) 对PNP-NS系统采用SP-RaNN逼近速度场，使速度近似逐点满足不可压缩约束。

Result: 理论方面：推导了原始未修正RaNN求解器的残差估计，给出了PNP系统原始外Picard迭代的条件局部收敛性结果，分析了值级正性修正、质量修正和SAV后处理步骤，建立了SP-RaNN空间的逼近定理及对应Oseen型问题的条件误差界。数值方面：在源驱动制造解测试中验证了近似精度，展示了值级正性修正、选定时刻质量匹配、基于规范固定势的自由能曲线计算以及基准测试中的无散度逼近效果。

Conclusion: SO-RaNN框架成功实现了对PNP和PNP-NS系统中关键物理约束（正性、质量守恒、能量耗散、无散度）的精确保持，同时具备理论收敛保证和良好的数值表现，为复杂多物理场耦合系统的神经网络求解提供了结构保持范式的有效范例。

Abstract: We develop a structure-oriented randomized neural network framework, termed SO-RaNN, for the Poisson-Nernst-Planck (PNP) system and the Poisson-Nernst-Planck-Navier-Stokes (PNP-NS) system. The decoupled linearized subproblems are solved iteratively by randomized neural networks in a space-time framework. For the concentration variables, a pointwise cut-off is used to enforce positivity at the value level, and discrete mass-scaling factors are computed at selected correction instants and interpolated in time, so as to ensure exact mass matching at those instants and to promote approximate mass preservation between them. To introduce an auxiliary discrete dissipation mechanism, we further employ an SAV-type post-processing correction, which yields monotonicity of the SAV auxiliary variable under the ideal SAV update. For the PNP-NS system, a structure-preserving randomized neural network (SP-RaNN) is used for the velocity field, so that the velocity approximation satisfies the incompressibility constraint pointwise by construction. On the theoretical side, we derive residual-based estimates for the raw, uncorrected RaNN solvers of the linearized subproblems, formulate a conditional local-in-time convergence result for the raw outer Picard iteration of the PNP system, and analyze the value-level positivity correction together with the mass-correction and SAV post-processing steps. For the PNP-NS system, we establish an approximation result for the SP-RaNN space and provide a conditional error statement for the corresponding linearized Oseen-type problem. Numerical experiments demonstrate approximation accuracy in the source-driven manufactured tests and illustrate the intended value-level positivity correction, selected-time mass matching, computed free-energy curves based on the final gauge-fixed potential, and divergence-free approximation in benchmark tests.

</details>


### [130] [A posteriori error bounds for pseudo-parabolic equations using $C_0$ semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20073)
*Martin Ossadnik,Torsten Linß*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对伪抛物型偏微分方程，推导了空间有限元法和时间BDF格式的后验误差界，基于C0半群理论和椭圆重构技术，并通过数值实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为伪抛物型偏微分方程的数值近似提供可靠的后验误差分析，填补该领域理论空白。

Method: 采用C0半群理论，并将椭圆重构概念推广到伪抛物型问题，结合有限元法（空间）和BDF格式（时间）推导后验误差界。

Result: 得到了空间和时间离散化的后验误差界，并通过数值实验验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文建立的后验误差界可用于指导自适应算法设计，椭圆重构技术成功扩展至伪抛物型问题。

Abstract: A class of pseudo-parabolic partial differential equations is considered. We derive a posteriori error bounds for approximations obtained by FEMs in space and a BDF formula in time. The analysis is based on the $C_0$ semigroup theory and an adaptation of the concept of elliptic reconstruction to pseudo-parabolic problems. The analysis is complemented with numerical experiments.

</details>


### [131] [A conservative adaptive rank method for the Wigner-Poisson system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20234)
*Andrew Christlieb,Sining Gong,F. Alejandro Padilla-Gomez,Jing-Mei Qiu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于1D1V Wigner-Poisson系统的保守自适应秩方法，通过结合基于采样的自适应秩更新与保守宏观修正（密度-动量局部修正+费米-狄拉克型重构+全局二次矩能量修正），在保持离散守恒量误差接近机器精度的同时，实现自适应秩压缩以降低相空间演化成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定性量子动力学模拟中，相空间演化计算成本高昂，且需保持宏观不变量以保证物理保真度，现有方法难以同时兼顾效率与守恒性。

Method: 将基于采样的自适应秩Wigner-Poisson更新与保守宏观修正相结合：1) 保守的密度-动量求解提供局部宏观更新；2) 费米-狄拉克型重构将宏观更新传递至动力学解；3) 全局二次矩修正强制满足离散总能量约束；4) 修正后的状态通过ACA SVD表示实现数值秩自适应。

Result: 对双流不稳定性、强朗道阻尼和Bump-on-tail不稳定性等算例的数值实验表明，方法在不同量子参数H下均能捕获基准Wigner-Poisson动力学，保持有界自适应秩，且全局离散不变量守恒误差接近机器精度。与全局质量-动量-能量保守公式的对比显示两者结果几乎一致。

Conclusion: 所提方法在1D1V周期性设定下能有效平衡自适应秩压缩与全局守恒性，两种修正策略均与自适应秩压缩兼容，为确定性量子动力学模拟提供了可行的降维保真方案。

Abstract: We propose a conservative adaptive rank method for the 1D1V Wigner-Poisson system. The method targets a central challenge in deterministic quantum kinetic simulations: reducing the cost of phase-space evolution while preserving the macroscopic invariants needed for physical fidelity. The scheme combines a sampling-based adaptive rank Wigner-Poisson update [7] with a conservative macroscopic correction. A conservative density-momentum solve provides local macroscopic updates, a Fermi-Dirac-type reconstruction transfers them to the kinetic solution, and a global quadratic moment correction enforces the discrete total energy constraint at the kinetic level. Unlike Maxwell-Boltzmann-type corrections commonly used in classical kinetic settings, the reconstruction uses a Fermi-Dirac-type form motivated by the model's quantum-statistical structure. The corrected state is incorporated into an ACA SVD representation, allowing the numerical rank to adapt to the phase-space complexity generated by the nonlocal Wigner operator and self-consistent Poisson field. Numerical experiments for the two-stream instability, strong Landau damping, and bump-on tail instability show that the method captures benchmark Wigner-Poisson dynamics for several values of the quantum parameter H, maintains bounded adaptive ranks, and preserves the specified global discrete invariants with conservation errors near machine precision. We also compare this formulation, which uses local density-momentum correction plus global total energy correction, with a related globally conservative formulation for mass, momentum, and energy [8]. The two approaches produce nearly identical phase-space and diagnostic results for the periodic benchmark test considered here, indicating that both correction strategies are compatible with adaptive rank compression for Wigner-Poisson dynamics in the tested 1D1V periodic setting.

</details>


### [132] [Data dependent Shepard approximation through and adaptive modification of the shape parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20332)
*José Kuruc,Juan Ruiz-Álvarez,Bo Wang,Dionisio-Félix Yáñez*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据依赖的Shepard插值方法，通过自适应调整权重减少跳跃间断处的模糊现象，理论证明了可行性，数值实验验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Shepard插值在数据存在跳跃间断时会产生模糊（smearing）现象，本文旨在借鉴[2]中针对径向基函数（RBF）插值的自适应策略，将该思想扩展到Shepard框架下，以减少间断处的模糊，同时保持光滑区域的高精度。

Method: 基于局部光滑性指标自适应调整Shepard插值的形状参数，从而改变影响权重。对于网格数据使用平方无差二阶差分，对于散乱数据使用平方最小二乘近似的Laplacian（经平均局部间距缩放）。靠近间断时，核函数行为类似于局部delta函数，从而减少模糊。

Result: 在理论上证明了该方法能够减少跳跃间断处的模糊现象；一维和二维的数值实验表明，该方法显著减少了间断处的模糊，同时在光滑区域保持高精度。

Conclusion: 数据依赖的Shepard插值方法能有效处理含跳跃间断的数据插值问题，兼具理论保障与良好的数值表现。

Abstract: In this article, we introduce a novel data-dependent Shepard interpolation method inspired by the adaptive strategies proposed in [2]. In this case, as Shepard interpolation does not produce oscillations, our approach has the core objective of reducing the smearing near jump discontinuities in the data in one and two dimensions. While the original work in [2] focuses in on Radial Basis Function (RBF) interpolation, we extend these ideas to the Shepard framework by incorporating a data-dependent adaptation mechanism. Specifically, we modify the classical Shepard interpolation by adaptively adjusting the influence weights based on local smoothness indicators that modify the shape parameter. These indicators, similar to those used in [2], are designed to detect discontinuities: for grid-based data, we use squared undivided second-order differences, and for scattered data, we employ squared least-squares approximations of the Laplacian scaled by the square of the mean local separation of stencil points. The resulting data-dependent weighting scheme forces the kernels close to a discontinuity to behave like a local delta function, effectively reducing the smearing of the discontinuities introduced by the classical Shepard approach. We establish the theoretical foundation of the method, including the properties of the new interpolation and we theoretically prove that the reduction of the smearing of discontinuities is possible. Numerical experiments in one and two dimensions confirm that the proposed data-dependent Shepard interpolation significantly reduces the smearing of jump discontinuities while maintaining high accuracy in smooth regions.

</details>


### [133] [Nonlinear Geotechnical Analysis Using a Polygonal Cell-Based Smoothed Finite Element Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20384)
*Mingjiao Yan,Yang Yang,Zongliang Zhang,Yinpeng Yin,Miao Zhang,Yijia Dong,Dong Pan,Xiaozi Lin,Tiankai Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于多边形单元的细胞平滑有限元法（CS-FEM），用于非线性岩土分析，并通过ABAQUS用户子程序实现。该方法结合Wachspress插值与细胞平滑技术，避免了多边形单元内形函数导数的直接计算，能统一处理标准多边形网格和带悬挂节点的混合四叉树网格。通过多个基准和工程实例验证，该方法在位移、应力、塑性应变、承载力及安全系数预测方面具有良好精度，同时提升了网格灵活性和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性岩土分析常涉及复杂几何、分期施工、局部破坏及网格相关的应力和塑性应变响应，现有方法在网格灵活性和计算效率方面存在不足，需要一种能统一处理多边形网格和带悬挂节点混合网格的数值方法。

Method: 将Wachspress插值与细胞平滑技术结合，通过多边形平滑子单元的边界积分计算平滑应变-位移矩阵，避免多边形单元内形函数导数的直接计算；通过增量弹塑性本构更新（含Mohr-Coulomb和Duncan-Chang模型）纳入非线性岩土材料行为；在ABAQUS中通过用户单元子程序实现。

Result: 通过带孔板、条形基础、心墙堆石坝、隧道开挖及边坡稳定性等算例验证，该方法能准确预测位移、应力、塑性应变、承载力及安全系数，且相比传统方法具有更好的网格灵活性和计算效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的多边形CS-FEM方法适用于非线性岩土分析，在精度和效率上均表现良好，为复杂岩土工程问题提供了一种有效的数值分析工具。

Abstract: Nonlinear geotechnical analysis often involves complex geometries, staged construction, local failure, and mesh-dependent stress and plastic strain responses. This study develops a polygonal cell-based smoothed finite element method (CS-FEM) for nonlinear geotechnical analysis and implements it in ABAQUS through the user element subroutine. The proposed method combines Wachspress interpolation with cell-based strain smoothing, in which the smoothed strain--displacement matrix is evaluated by boundary integration over polygonal smoothing subcells. This formulation avoids direct calculation of shape-function derivatives inside polygonal elements and enables standard polygonal meshes and hybrid quadtree meshes with hanging nodes to be handled in a unified framework. Nonlinear geomaterial behavior is incorporated through incremental elasto-plastic constitutive updates, including the Mohr--Coulomb model and the Duncan--Chang model. Several benchmark and engineering examples, including a perforated plate, strip footing, core rockfill dam, tunnel excavation, and slope stability problems, are presented for verification. The results show that the proposed method accurately predicts displacement, stress, plastic strain, bearing capacity, and factor of safety, while providing improved mesh flexibility and computational efficiency for nonlinear geotechnical analysis.

</details>


### [134] [CoarseSolvers for Exascale Solution of Poisson Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20496)
*Thilina Ratnayaka,Paul Fischer,Luke Olson*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种两层Schwarz方法替代代数多重网格(AMG)作为p-多重网格(pMG)预处理器的粗层求解器，用于求解不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程离散化产生的压力Poisson方程。该方法包含局部问题和全局粗问题，主要贡献是设计了一种新颖、结构化、非嵌套的全局粗空间，实现了通信自由的插值操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用结构化、非嵌套的粗空间替代AMG作为pMG预条件子的粗层求解器，以提高可扩展性和计算效率。

Method: 提出两层Schwarz方法：(1) 原始pMG粗空间中的局部问题；(2) 全局粗问题。全局粗空间采用新颖的结构化非嵌套粗空间，支持通信自由的插值操作。

Result: 在Summit/Frontier超级计算机上使用Nek5000/RS求解器进行实验，证明了所提方法相比BoomerAMG的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的结构化非嵌套粗空间方法能有效替代AMG作为pMG的粗层求解器，在高端超算平台上表现优于当前最先进的AMG求解器。

Abstract: WepresentatwolevelSchwarzmethodasanalternativetoAlgebraicMultigridmethod(AMG) used as the last level (coarse) solver of the p-multigrid pMG preconditioner for pressure Poission equation resulting from Spectral/Finite element descretization of incompressible Navier-Stokes eqaution. Proposed Schwarz method consits of a local problem in the original pMG coarse space and a global coarse problem. Main contribution of the paper is a novel, structured and a non-nested coarse space for the global coarse problem. Structured nature of the proposed global coarse space enable communication-free interpolation between the original p-multgrid coarse space and the global coarse problem. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method compared to the state of the art AMG solver BoomerAMG by a series of experiments performed using Nek5000/RS, a suite of highly scalable incompressible Navier-Stokes solvers, on Summit/Frontier supercomputers at Oak Ridge Leadership Computing Facility.

</details>
