### [135] [Scintillation of the first-known pulsar planetary system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19406)
*J. M. Yao,L. Zhang,A. Wolszczan,William A. Coles,D. Li,Richard N. Manchester,N. Wang,C. H. Niu,P. Wang,F. F. Kou,J. P. Yuan*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 利用FAST望远镜对首个已知脉冲星行星系统PSR B1257+12进行闪烁研究，通过31次观测分析了闪烁时标、带宽和频率漂移率，检测到内、中、外三层弧结构，确定了各散射屏的距离和速度，发现色散量变化主要由远离脉冲星的等离子体主导，脉冲星周围环境相对干净。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究首个已知脉冲星行星系统PSR B1257+12的星际闪烁特性，以揭示其周围环境及星际介质的结构。

Method: 使用FAST望远镜进行31次观测（≥30分钟），对其中14次较长观测（≥120分钟）进行一维自相关函数分析得到闪烁时标、带宽和频率漂移率；通过二次谱分析检测内、中、外弧结构；利用年调制分析内弧曲率确定散射屏距离和横向速度；通过时延轮廓分析确定谱指数。

Result: 12个历元测得闪烁参数；2次观测因AU尺度结构干扰无法测量；发现内、中、外三层弧同时存在的证据；内弧曲率年调制表明各向同性散射，散射屏距离233±28 pc，横向速度V_scr,α=-7.16±2.16 km/s，V_scr,δ=-41.07±5.69 km/s；中、外弧对应的屏-脉冲星距离分别为354±22 pc和166±12 pc；DM变化主要由远离脉冲星的等离子体主导；脉冲星周围环境相对干净。

Conclusion: PSR B1257+12周围环境可能相对干净，色散量变化主要来自远端等离子体；低DM变化率及缺乏近端散射屏支持这一结论；但不能排除存在未检测到的近端散射屏，需要更高灵敏度或更长时间的观测来验证。

Abstract: We present a scintillation study of the first-known pulsar planetary system, PSR~B1257+12, using the Five-hundred-meter Aperture Spherical radio Telescope (FAST). A total of 31 observations with durations greater than or equal to 30 minutes were analyzed. For 14 longer observations (greater than or equal to 120 minutes), one-dimensional autocorrelation function analyses yielded the scintillation timescale, scintillation bandwidth, and frequency-drift rate for 12 epochs. Two observations show strong periodic modulation in the frequency-domain auto-correlation function, likely caused by astronomical-unit-scale structures along the propagation path, preventing reliable measurements of the scintillation timescale and bandwidth. In three observations, secondary spectra reveal simultaneous detections of inner, middle, and outer arcs. Analysis of the annual modulation of the inner-arc curvature indicates isotropic scattering, with a screen distance of $233\pm28$~pc and transverse velocity $V_{\rm scr,α}=-7.16\pm2.16$ km~s$^{-1}$, $V_{\rm scr,δ}=-41.07\pm5.69$ km~s$^{-1}$. Delay-profile analysis for both the inner and outer arcs suggest spectral exponents consistent with, or smaller than, the Kolmogorov value. Under isotropic scattering, the screen--pulsar distances are $354\pm22$~pc and $166\pm12$~pc for the middle and outer arcs. Combining the results from long-term timing analyses with our scintillation measurements, we find that the dispersion measure (DM) variations are primarily dominated by plasma located further away from the pulsar. The low DM-change rate of the outer arc and the absence of nearby scattering screens suggest that the immediate environment of the pulsar may be relatively clean. Alternatively, scattering screens closer to the pulsar may exist but remain undetected, requiring higher-sensitivity or longer-duration observations.

</details>


### [136] [Evidence for protostellar jets as a population of hadronic gamma-ray sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19445)
*Javier Méndez-Gallego,Rubén López-Coto,Emma de Oña Wilhelmi,Stefano Menchiari,Iván Agudo,Rubén Fedriani*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文报道了来自年轻恒星天体（原恒星）种群的高能伽马射线统计显著探测，揭示了一类银河系"伽马亮原恒星"。观测表明，伽马射线起源于原恒星喷流中加速的质子与周围分子云相互作用产生的π⁰介子衰变，且宇宙线输出与辐射光度相关，表明粒子加速与系统机械功率成比例。这一发现打开了研究原恒星非热过程的新窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上原恒星被视为纯热辐射源，虽然射电同步辐射喷流暗示了相对论性电子的存在，但质子加速的直接证据一直缺失。本文旨在寻找原恒星中质子加速的观测证据。

Method: 对年轻恒星天体种群进行伽马射线统计显著性探测，分析伽马射线与辐射光度的相关性，并基于π⁰介子衰变机制解释伽马射线产生过程。

Result: 统计显著地探测到来自原恒星种群的伽马射线，发现宇宙线输出与辐射光度之间存在相关性，表明粒子加速与系统机械功率标度相关。

Conclusion: 发现了一类新的银河系伽马射线源——伽马亮原恒星，揭示了原恒星喷流中质子加速的存在。这为研究恒星形成过程中的吸积、喷流和反馈机制提供了新的观测窗口，有助于理解年轻恒星注入环境的能量反馈如何塑造后续恒星和行星形成的条件。

Abstract: Stars are born in darkness, deep within cold, dense molecular clouds where gravity drives the collapse of gas and dust, giving rise to protostars, the earliest stages of stellar evolution. Once considered purely thermal sources, these young systems are now emerging as sites of energetic non-thermal activity. While radio synchrotron jets hinted at the presence of relativistic electrons, direct confirmation of proton acceleration remained elusive. Here we report a statistically significant detection of gamma rays from a population of young stellar objects, revealing a Galactic class of Gamma-Loud Protostars. Observations point towards particle acceleration within protostellar jets, where gamma-ray emission arises from protons interacting with surrounding molecular clouds via pion decay. We find a correlation between cosmic-ray output and bolometric luminosity, suggesting that particle acceleration scales with the system's mechanical power. These findings open a new observational window into the role of non-thermal processes in protostellar evolution and suggest that gamma-ray studies of protostars can provide critical insights into accretion, ejection, and feedback in star formation. This previously overlooked emission traces the energetic feedback that young stars inject into their surroundings, shaping the conditions for subsequent star and planet formation.

</details>


### [137] [Damping of Fast Radio Bursts in the Inner Magnetospheres of Magnetars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19448)
*Siddhant Solanki,Jens Mahlmann,Alexander Philippov,Andrei Beloborodov*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过三维力自由电动力学模拟，研究快速射电暴（FRB）在磁星磁层中的传播，发现FRB会激发阿尔芬涨落，导致强非线性衰减。在静态偶极磁层中，非线性衰减在~10-100个磁星半径内有效；在磁层爆发期间的相对论性磁流出中，三波相互作用效率更高，将逃逸半径约束在≥10²-10³个磁星半径。结果证实非线性等离子体过程严重限制了FRB从磁星内磁层的逃逸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究FRB在磁星磁层中的传播行为，特别是非线性等离子体过程（如三波相互作用）对射电波能量传输和逃逸的制约作用。

Method: 采用三维力自由电动力学模拟，研究FRB在静态偶极磁层和磁层爆发期间的相对论性磁流出两种场景下的传播与衰减特性。

Result: 在静态偶极磁层中，非线性衰减在~10-100个磁星半径内有效；在磁层爆发期间，三波相互作用效率更高，逃逸半径需≥10²-10³个磁星半径。

Conclusion: 非线性等离子体过程（如阿尔芬涨落激发和三波相互作用）强烈限制FRB从磁星内磁层的逃逸，对FRB起源模型提供了重要约束。

Abstract: We investigate the propagation of fast radio bursts (FRBs) through magnetar magnetospheres. Previous work showed that, in the inner magnetosphere, GHz radio waves propagate as fast magnetosonic waves and undergo resonant three-wave interactions that transfer their energy into trapped Alfvén waves. Using three-dimensional force-free electrodynamics simulations, we demonstrate that FRBs would excite Alfvénic fluctuations, leading to strong nonlinear attenuation of the radio signal. In quiescent dipolar magnetospheres, the nonlinear decay stays efficient within $\sim10$--$100$ magnetar radii; charge starvation of the excited Alfvén waves stops the decay at larger radii. For FRBs propagating within relativistic magnetic outflows launched during magnetospheric eruptions, three-wave interactions remain efficient and constrain the escape radius to $\gtrsim10^2$--$10^3$ magnetar radii for luminous bursts. Our results confirm that nonlinear plasma processes strongly limit the escape of FRBs from the inner magnetospheres of magnetars.

</details>


### [138] [Nonlinear Decay of Fast Magnetosonic Waves through Weak Turbulence: Force-Free Electrodynamics Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19450)
*Siddhant Solanki,Jens Mahlmann,Alexander Philippov*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过相对论无力电动力学模拟，研究了快速磁声波（FMS）在强磁化环境（如磁陀星磁层）中的传播，发现FMS波会通过参数衰变不稳定性高效非线性转换为次级FMS波和阿尔文波，导致能量耗散和频谱展宽，因此FMS波难以在无显著耗散的情况下从磁陀星磁层逃逸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速射电暴（FRB）潜在产生于磁陀星内磁层，FMS波能否在不被显著改造的情况下逃逸是一个未决问题，本文旨在探究这一传播机制。

Method: 使用相对论无力电动力学模拟，验证Golbraikh & Lyubarsky (2023)的理论预测，研究低频FMS波在强磁化环境中的非线性演化。

Result: FMS波经参数衰变不稳定性高效转换为次级FMS波和阿尔文波；即使在FMS与阿尔文分量达到近似能量均分后，能量耗散仍持续进行；激发波谱广泛，覆盖模拟域内惯性范围的大部分k空间。

Conclusion: FMS波很可能无法在无显著耗散和频谱展宽的情况下从磁陀星磁层逃逸。

Abstract: We investigate the propagation of low-frequency fast-magnetosonic (FMS) waves in highly magnetized environments. Such conditions are relevant to the escape of GHz fast radio bursts potentially produced in the inner magnetospheres of magnetars. It remains an open question whether such waves can escape without substantial reprocessing. Using relativistic force-free electrodynamics simulations, we confirm the key theoretical predictions of Golbraikh & Lyubarsky (2023) and demonstrate that FMS waves undergo efficient nonlinear conversion into secondary FMS and Alfvén waves via the parametric decay instability. This process continues to drain energy from the primary FMS waves even after approximate energy equipartition between the FMS and Alfvén components is established. The resulting spectrum of excited waves is broad, extending across much of the inertial range in $k$-space within the simulation domain. Our results indicate that FMS waves likely do not escape magnetar magnetospheres without substantial dissipation and spectral broadening.

</details>


### [139] [Short-Range Forces Can Catalyze Extreme Orbital Evolution in Hierarchical Triples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19456)
*Ygal Y. Klein,Chris Hamilton*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文发现，在双星系统中，短程力（如相对论和潮汐形变）在非双平均近似下非但不会抑制冯·蔡佩尔-利多夫-科扎伊效应导致的极端偏心率激发，反而会通过非绝热跳跃催化这种极端行为，使双星探索到更广的相空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统双平均近似认为短程力会抑制ZLK效应导致的偏心率激发，但本研究想检验当放宽双平均近似时，短程力的实际作用是否与现有认知不同。

Method: 在试验粒子四极矩层次下，放松双平均近似，研究短程力（相对论、潮汐/旋转形变）对三元系统中内双星轨道演化的影响，分析非绝热跳跃对绝热不变量和角动量分量的演化。

Result: 短程力在非双平均近似下会催化而非抑制极端偏心率行为；短程力可驱动内双星有效绝热不变量发生大的离散跳跃，显著改变最大/最小偏心率和 secular 周期；角动量分量 j_z 出现长期演化（首次在四极矩三体机制中被发现）；双星探索的相空间比任何已知（半）解析ZLK理论隐含的范围更广。

Conclusion: 短程力在非双平均近似下成为极端偏心率行为的催化剂，而非抑制因素；该效应在四极矩层次即可出现，伴随论文进一步展示结合八极矩演化时更为极端的行为。

Abstract: Hierarchical triples are promising environments for producing exotica such as black hole mergers and hot Jupiters, because of the von Zeipel-Lidov-Kozai (ZLK) effect, whereby a distant tertiary can torque an inner binary to high eccentricity over secular timescales. In the double-averaged (DA) approximation to ZLK, this eccentricity excitation is suppressed by apsidal precession due to `short-range forces' (SRFs) like relativity and tidal/rotational bulges. Here we show that, when the DA approximation is relaxed, SRFs often catalyze, rather than suppress, extreme eccentricity behavior. This occurs because SRFs can drive large, discrete jumps in the binary's effective `adiabatic invariants' during high-eccentricity episodes. These nonadiabatic jumps can dramatically alter the maximum/minimum eccentricity and secular period of astrophysically relevant triples, including some for which SRFs were previously thought irrelevant. Even the angular momentum component $j_z$ evolves secularly -- to our knowledge, this is the first time such evolution has been demonstrated from a quadrupole-order, three-body mechanism. In short, binaries may explore much more of phase space than is implied by any (semi-)analytic ZLK theory of which we are aware. We demonstrate this at the test-particle quadrupole level; in a companion paper we show how even more-extreme behavior occurs when the jumps are combined with octupolar ZLK evolution.

</details>


### [140] [On the Contribution of Local Sources to the Galactic Cosmic-Ray Spectrum: An Exact Series Solution for Two-Zone Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19701)
*Zi-Hang Liu,Yiwei Bao,Ruo-Yu Liu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了宇宙线质子和氦核能谱在膝盖区域以下偏离简单幂律的多TeV结构现象。通过蒙特卡洛模拟，发现在本地源附近存在慢扩散区域（双区模型）时，最强本地源贡献与背景相当的概率从均匀扩散模型的0.4%增加到1.7-2.2%。然而，即使考虑近源输运抑制，统计困难仍然存在。对候选超新星遗迹的分析表明，复现10 TeV特征需要额外假设，特别是更硬的本地注入谱和有利的扩散系数。因此，本地源解释虽然可能但高度依赖模型，需要独立约束源注入历史、粒子输运机制和局部星际湍流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙线质子与氦谱在膝盖以下区域（多TeV能段）观测到偏离简单幂律的结构，一种解释是邻近天体源贡献了额外成分。但先前研究显示，在均匀扩散模型下，本地源主导的概率极低。本文旨在探讨当考虑宇宙线在源附近经历低效输运（即慢扩散区域）时，这一概率如何变化。

Method: 推导了一个级数格林函数，用于快速计算慢扩散区域存在时的粒子分布；基于该函数进行银河系源群体的蒙特卡洛模拟，比较均匀扩散与双区（内慢外快）扩散模型下的概率差异；同时分析了编录中的邻近超新星遗迹候选体。

Result: 均匀扩散模型中，最强本地源在10 TeV与背景相当的概率约0.4%；双区模型中该概率增至1.7-2.2%。对编录中邻近候选超新星遗迹的分析表明，复现10 TeV特征需要额外假设，特别是更硬的本地注入谱和有利的扩散系数。不同粒子输运模型下，给定源的预测贡献差异显著。

Conclusion: 近源慢扩散效应会削弱（但未消除）利用单一或少数邻近源解释多TeV谱结构的统计困难。本地源解释是可行的，但高度依赖模型参数，需要独立约束源注入历史、粒子输运机制和局部星际湍流。

Abstract: Measurements of cosmic-ray proton and helium spectra below the knee show deviations from simple power laws, including multi-TeV structures. A possible explanation is that one or a few nearby sources contribute an additional component to the local spectrum. However, previous study shows that a dominant local contribution is statistically unlikely under a homogeneous diffusion model. In this work, we investigate how this probability changes if cosmic rays experience inefficient transport near their sources, motivated by observations of extended gamma-ray emission around Galactic accelerators. We derive a series Green's function that enables fast calculation of the particle distribution in this scenario, making Monte Carlo calculations for Galactic source populations feasible. The inner slow-diffusion region delays escape and redistributes the arriving particles in time and energy. In Monte Carlo realizations, the probability that the strongest local source becomes comparable to the background at $10\,\rm{TeV}$ increases from about $0.4\%$ in homogeneous diffusion to $1.7$--$2.2\%$ in the two-zone models. Thus inhibited near-source transport weakens, but does not remove, the statistical difficulty. We then examine cataloged nearby candidate supernova remnants and show that a $10\,\rm{TeV}$ feature can be reproduced only with additional assumptions, especially a harder local injection spectrum and a favorable diffusion coefficient. The predicted contribution of a given source changes strongly among different particle transport model. Therefore, the local source interpretations are plausible but highly model dependent, and require independent constraints on source injection history, particle transport mechanisms, and local interstellar turbulence.

</details>


### [141] [On the braking index evolution of PSR B0540-69: wind braking of pulsars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19856)
*H. Tong*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文分析了PSR B0450-69脉冲星的自转减慢指数变化，发现其在高自转减慢态下的制动指数逐渐增大并趋近先前预测值，提出瞬态变化可能源于粒子数密度的微小变化，最终制动指数呈指数演化形式趋近稳态值，并给出了脉冲星风制动唯象模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解PSR B0450-69脉冲星在自转减慢状态变化后制动指数随时间增大的物理机制，并验证先前关于增强粒子风导致制动指数变小的预测。

Method: 通过观测PSR B0450-69的制动指数时间演化数据，与先前粒子风模型的理论预测进行对比分析，并建立制动指数指数演化形式的唯象模型，同时推广到风制动模型的一般化处理。

Result: 当前测量的制动指数正接近先前理论预测值；制动指数的瞬态变化可归因于粒子数密度的微小变化分量；制动指数最终演化呈现指数形式；PSR B0540-69的制动指数预计将趋近某个稳定值。

Conclusion: 未来制动指数测量将有助于阐明制动指数演化的物理机制，检验脉冲星磁层中不同的粒子加速势模型；所提出的风制动唯象模型可简化对脉冲星制动指数、间歇脉冲星及PSR B0540-69的应用。

Abstract: The pulsar PSR B0450-69 has both a braking index measurement and spin-down state change. After its spin-down state change, it shows an increasing braking index with time. Previously, it is pointed out that the spin-down state change may be caused by an enhanced particle wind. The prediction is that its braking index in the high spin-down state will be smaller. The current measured braking index is approaching the previous prediction. The transient variation of braking index may be due to a small varying part of the particle number density. The final braking index evolution is found to be in an exponential form. The braking index of PSR B0540-69 is expected to approach some steady value. Future braking index measurement may make clear the physics for the braking index evolution. It may also help to make test different particle acceleration potential in the pulsar magnetosphere. Finally, a phenomenological treatment of wind braking model of pulsars is presented. It can simplify the applications to pulsar braking index, intermittent pulsars and PSR B0540-69.

</details>


### [142] [Accreting stellar-mass black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19952)
*Greg Marcel,Bailey Tetarenko,Adam Ingram,Tom Maccarone,Alexandra Veledina,Phil Charles*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文综述了吸积恒星级黑洞在电磁波段的剧烈变异性，包括谱态转变、爆发和喷流产生，重点关注连续谱发射、时变特性、发射线和X射线偏振，并讨论了未来多波段、时域和偏振研究的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恒星级黑洞吸积系统表现出丰富的电磁变异性，是理解强引力场中吸积过程的独特实验室，但谱态转变的物理起源、磁场在驱动外流和塑造吸积流中的作用、盘不稳定性与喷流发射之间的联系等基本问题仍未解决。

Method: 本文采用综述方法，系统梳理了恒星级黑洞吸积系统在连续谱、时变（准周期振荡和宽带噪声）、发射线（多波段发射线及X射线铁K线相对论性反射）和X射线偏振方面的最新研究进展。

Result: 发现了吸积盘、冕和喷流之间的复杂相互作用主导了谱态与时变行为；铁K线可探测内盘性质；偏振研究表明硬态下冕可能垂直于喷流轴延伸，但软态偏振观测与简单理论预期不符。

Conclusion: 尽管取得了显著进展，但谱态转变的物理机制、磁场对吸积流和外流的调控、盘不稳定性与喷流发射的关联等根本问题仍有待解决，未来需要多波段、时域和偏振测量的协同研究。

Abstract: Accreting stellar-mass black holes exhibit dramatic variability across the electromagnetic spectrum, including spectral state transitions, outbursts, and jet production, making them unique laboratories for understanding accretion processes in strong gravitational fields. This review synthesizes recent progress in understanding these systems, focusing on their continuum emission, timing properties, emission lines, and X-ray polarization. A complex interplay between the accretion disk, the so-called corona, and jet underlies the observed spectral and timing behavior, with quasi-periodic oscillations and broadband noise providing windows into the dynamics of the innermost accretion flow. Emission lines across all wavelengths serve as critical diagnostics of disk structure, outflows, and reprocessing, while iron K lines in the X-ray band probe the properties of the inner disk through relativistic reflection. Polarization studies suggest that the corona is likely extended perpendicular to the jet axis in the hard state, while the soft state remains poorly understood, with observations that do not yet conform to simple theoretical expectations; a puzzle that continues to challenge our interpretation of accretion geometry. Despite significant advances, fundamental questions remain about the physical origins of state transitions, the role of magnetic fields in driving outflows and shaping the accretion flow, and the connection between disk instabilities and jet launching. This review underscores the need for future multi-wavelength, timing, and polarimetric studies to deepen our understanding of accretion physics in strong-gravity environments.

</details>


### [143] [Supernova Remnants in the IXPE era: a review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19979)
*Riccardo Ferrazzoli,IXPE Collaboration*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: IXPE对五个年轻超新星遗迹的X射线偏振观测揭示了5%-30%+的偏振度，发现了径向和切向两种磁场形态，挑战了传统无线电观测的二分法，表明激波速度和环境密度共同塑造磁场拓扑结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用IXPE提供的首个空间分辨X射线偏振测量，直接探测超新星遗迹中有效粒子加速区域的磁场几何与湍流，以约束扩散激波加速和磁场放大模型。

Method: IXPE对五个已发表结果的年轻超新星遗迹（Cas A、Tycho、SN 1006、RX J1713.7-3946、Vela Jr.）进行空间分辨X射线偏振观测，测量偏振度和磁场方向。

Result: 所有遗迹均表现出显著偏振（5%-30%+）；Cas A、Tycho、SN 1006呈径向磁场，RX J1713.7-3946和Vela Jr.呈切向磁场，且此双重行为与演化阶段无简单关联。

Conclusion: IXPE结果挑战了传统无线电观测建立的磁场二分法，揭示激波速度和星周介质密度是塑造磁场拓扑的关键因素，为理解年轻超新星遗迹激波中的宇宙线加速过程提供了重要约束。

Abstract: The Imaging X-ray Polarimetry Explorer (IXPE) has opened a new observational window on the physics of supernova remnants (SNRs) by providing the first spatially resolved X-ray polarimetry measurements. These data directly probe the geometry and turbulence of magnetic fields in regions of efficient particle acceleration, thereby constraining models of diffusive shock acceleration and magnetic-field amplification. IXPE has so far observed six young SNRs (Cas A, Tycho, SN 1006, RX J1713.7-3946, Vela Jr., and RCW 86) with published results on the first five. The observations reveal significant polarization in all cases, with degrees of polarization ranging from 5% to over 30%, reflecting different turbulence levels and environmental conditions. Three remnants (Cas A, Tycho, and SN 1006) show predominantly radial magnetic fields, while RX J1713.7-3946 and Vela Jr. display tangential morphologies. This dual behavior, not simply correlated with evolutionary stage, challenges the long-standing dichotomy inferred from radio observations and suggests that both shock velocity and circumstellar medium density play key roles in shaping magnetic-field topology. IXPE's results mark a major step toward disentangling the processes governing cosmic-ray acceleration in young SNR shocks, with ongoing and future observations expected to further constrain the interplay between turbulence, shock dynamics, and particle acceleration.

</details>


### [144] [A Whisper from Within: Response of a Pulsar Timing Array to an Internal Gravitational-wave Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20021)
*Houyuan Qi,Xian Chen,Lin Wang,Kuo Liu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本论文研究了位于球状星团或星系核内的毫秒脉冲星构成的微型脉冲星计时阵列（mini-PTA）对内部纳赫兹引力波源的探测能力。由于阵列尺度与引力波波长相当，传统的平面波近似失效，需要考虑近场效应（波前曲率、非辐射自场及引力波对脉冲星的直接扰动）。作者建立了综合模型计算计时残差，发现在足够接近引力波源（几个波长内）时，非辐射场主导使残差随距离按1/r_e^2至1/r_e^4急剧上升，远强于远场的1/r_e标度。在M15、ω Centauri、Sgr A*和M31等真实天体配置中，残差可达微秒至毫秒量级，处于现有射电望远镜探测范围内。这表明mini-PTA可有效探测中等质量及超大质量黑洞双星等不可见引力波源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统脉冲星计时阵列依赖远场平面波近似探测银河系外超大质量黑洞双星产生的引力波，但忽略了位于球状星团或星系核内的毫秒脉冲星阵列（mini-PTA）对内部引力波源的探测潜力。当引力波源位于阵列内部时，阵列尺度与波长相当，近场效应（波前曲率、非辐射自场、引力波对脉冲星的直接扰动）不可忽略，现有模型无法准确描述此情形下的计时残差。因此需要建立包含近场效应的综合模型，评估mini-PTA探测内部纳赫兹引力波源的可行性。

Method: 作者建立了包含近场效应的综合模型来计算mini-PTA中由内部引力波源产生的脉冲计时残差。模型考虑了波前曲率、引力波源的非辐射自场以及引力波对脉冲星的直接扰动。针对球状星团（M15和ω Centauri）和星系核（Sgr A*和M31）中的真实引力波源配置进行了数值计算，分析了不同距离下计时残差的量级和标度行为。

Result: 当毫秒脉冲星位于引力波源几个波长内时，计时残差可达1微秒（球状星团）至毫秒量级（星系中心），处于现有射电望远镜的探测范围内。非辐射场在近距离处主导，导致残差随距离按1/r_e^2至1/r_e^4急剧上升，远高于传统远场近似的1/r_e标度。

Conclusion: 球状星团或星系核中的mini-PTA可作为探测内部不可见引力波源（包括中等质量和超大质量黑洞双星）的有力工具，其近场效应显著增强了对近距离源的探测灵敏度。

Abstract: Millisecond pulsars (MSPs) are abundant in globular clusters (GCs) and probably also in galactic nuclei. They offer the potential to form a miniature pulsar timing array (mini-PTA) to detect nanohertz gravitational-wave (GW) sources located inside the array. Since the size of such an array is comparable to the wavelength of GW, the conventional plane-wave approximation becomes invalid, and near-field effects, including wavefront curvature, non-radiative self-field of the GW source, and direct perturbation of pulsar by GW, become significant. In this work, we incorporate these effects in a comprehensive model to calculate the timing residual induced by a GW source inside a mini-PTA. We also consider realistic GW source configurations in GCs (M15 and $ω$ Centauri) and in galactic nuclei (Sgr A* and M31), and find that for MSPs located sufficiently close to the GW source (within a few wavelengths), the residual can reach $1~μ\mathrm{s}$ in GCs and up to milliseconds in galactic centers, within the potential detection reach of current radio telescopes. Crucially, when the pulsar lies within a few GW wavelengths of the source, the non-radiative field dominates and causes the residual to rise much more steeply (between $1/r_e^2$ and $1/r_e^4$, where $r_e$ is the distance to the source) than the conventional far-field scaling ($1/r_e$). These results demonstrate that mini-PTAs in GCs or galactic nuclei can serve as powerful probes of otherwise invisible GW sources, including intermediate-mass and supermassive black hole binaries.

</details>


### [145] [A FAST search for radio pulsations during the dormant state of the AMSPs IGR J00291+5934 and MAXI J1957+032](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20086)
*A. Marino,E. Parent,F. Coti Zelati,M. C. Baglio,A. Papitto,A. Sanna,A. Anitra,C. Kazantsev,N. Rea,A. Borghese,L. Burderi,T. Di Salvo,C. Espinoza,X. Hou,R. Iaria,G. Illiano,D. M. Russell,R. Sathyaprakash*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用FAST望远镜对两个吸积毫秒脉冲星（IGR J00291+5934和MAXI J1957+032）在宁静态进行了深度射电搜寻，未探测到相干周期性信号，给出了迄今最严格的脉冲射电流密度上限（分别为3.3 μJy和5.6 μJy）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 吸积毫秒脉冲星（AMSP）在宁静态是否像过渡毫秒脉冲星（tMSP）一样发出射电脉冲信号尚不清楚，此前除一个特例外均未探测到，本研究旨在通过更高灵敏度的FAST望远镜进行深度搜寻以检验这一可能性。

Method: 使用FAST望远镜L波段（1-1.5 GHz）对IGR J00291+5934和MAXI J1957+032进行观测；同时利用Swift和Las Cumbres天文台获取X射线和光学上限以确认宁静态；进行相干周期性的深度搜索，并基于10%脉冲占空比设定射电流密度上限。

Result: 未在已知自旋频率或其他频率处探测到显著候选信号；对IGR J00291+5934和MAXI J1957+032分别得到3.3 μJy和5.6 μJy的脉冲射电流密度上限，这是迄今对任何已知持久型AMSP最严格的限制。

Conclusion: 尽管灵敏度大幅提升，仍未在AMSP宁静态探测到射电脉冲信号，暗示AMSP与tMSP在宁静态的射电行为存在本质差异，可能与其磁场结构、吸积历史或脉冲星机制激活条件不同有关。

Abstract: Accreting millisecond pulsars (AMSPs) and transitional millisecond pulsars (tMSPs) are neutron star low-mass X-ray binaries which can evolve into "recycled" radio millisecond pulsars. In both types of systems, X-ray pulsations have been detected during phases of X-ray activity when matter accretion through a disc is turned on. On the other hand, when accretion stops, and these systems enter the quiescent, low-luminosity X-ray state, only tMSPs become visible as radio pulsars. Despite several attempts, radio pulsations have never been detected in quiescent AMSPs, except for IGR J18245$-$2452. In this manuscript, we present the results of two observational campaigns performed on the AMSPs IGR J00291+5934 and MAXI J1957+032 with the Five-hundred-meter Aperture Spherical Telescope ($\it{FAST}$) in L-band (1-1.5 GHz). Both sources have most likely been observed in quiescence, as suggested by the upper limits on their X-ray and optical flux obtained with Swift and the Las Cumbres Observatory, respectively. We have performed a deep search for coherent periodicities in radio but found no significant candidate signal, either at the known spin frequency of those sources or at other frequencies. Assuming a pulse duty cycle of 10%, we derive upper limits on the pulsed radio flux density of 3.3 $μ$Jy and 5.6 $μ$Jy for IGR J00291+5934 and MAXI J1957+032, respectively, which are the most stringent limits so far for any known persistent AMSP.

</details>


### [146] [On the impact of the carbon fusion rate over the properties of superbursts -- Numerical simulations of superbursts with MESA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20088)
*Martin Nava-Callejas,Stéphane Goriely,Nicolas Chamel*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了碳聚变反应速率的变化对中子星超爆发物理特性的影响，发现速率提高1000倍会缩短复发时间、衰变时间和点火柱深，并影响α核素合成，其效果与降低包层底部加热相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来碳聚变速率被重新审视，发现在超爆发温度（T≤10^9 K）下可能比经典速率（Caughler & Fowler 1988）高或低1000倍，因此需探究这种变化对超爆发物理的影响。

Method: 使用公共代码MESA v.24.08.1，结合四个版本的碳聚变反应速率进行超爆发模拟。

Result: 碳聚变速率提高1000倍（T≤10^9 K）会缩短超爆发的复发时间和衰变时间，降低点火柱深；速率降低1000倍则相反。爆炸最高温度也受此影响，进而增强或减弱α核素的合成。这些变化与减少包层底部加热量的效果相当。

Conclusion: 碳聚变速率的变化对超爆发关键物理参数有显著影响，且与底部加热效应存在可比性，为理解超爆发机制提供了新的约束。

Abstract: Context: Superbursts are very energetic explosions in the crust of neutron stars in Low-Mass X-ray Binaries (LMXBs). These are triggered by unstable carbon burning at $T\leq 10^{9}$ K. In recent years, there has been a re-examination of the carbon fusion rate, finding that at these temperatures it might be either smaller or higher with respect to the classic rate from Caughler \& Fowler (1988) by a factor $10^{3}$. Aims: We explore the consequences changing the carbon fusion rate has over the physics of superbursts. Methods: For simulating superbursts, we employ the public code MESA v.24.08.1, as well as four versions of the carbon fusion reaction rate. Results: An enhancement of the reaction rate by a factor $10^{3}$ at $T\leq 10^9$~K reduces the recurrence and decay times of the superburst, as well as the column depth at ignition. The opposite behavior is observed when the carbon fusion rate is reduced by the same factor. The maximum temperature reached during the explosion is also sensitive to these changes, leading to either an enhancement or a reduction in the synthesis of $α$-nuclides. These changes are comparable to the effect of reducing the amount of base heating at the bottom of the envelope.

</details>


### [147] [Kick bimodality of neutron stars and mode dependence of their parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20201)
*Anton D. Lazarev,Sergei B. Popov*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 通过对约200颗正常孤立射电脉冲星的分析，发现约30%属于低速度模式，并揭示了两类脉冲星在磁场分布上的显著差异：低磁场脉冲星（B≲10¹² G）在低速度模式中占比更高，而B≲10¹¹ G的脉冲星中未发现高速度模式成员。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星的初始速度分布（natal velocity kick）对于理解超新星爆炸不对称性和中子星演化至关重要。先前研究暗示其可能呈双峰分布，但缺乏基于大样本观测数据的系统性分类与验证。

Method: 利用约200颗具有精确自转和运动学参数的正常孤立射电脉冲星，根据双峰速度分布模型将其划分为低速度模式或高速度模式，然后比较两组在特征年龄、距离、射电光度、脉冲宽度和磁场等参数上的差异，并分析选择效应的影响。

Result: 约30%的脉冲星属于低速度模式；特征年龄、距离和射电光度在两组间存在差异，但可归因于选择偏差；脉冲宽度（不受强选择效应影响）无显著差异；低速度模式中低磁场脉冲星（≲10¹² G）占比更高，且B≲10¹¹ G的脉冲星中完全没有高速度模式成员。

Conclusion: 中子星速度分布与磁场之间存在关联，低磁场脉冲星倾向于具有较低的速度。该差异的物理起源尚不明确，可能涉及超新星爆炸机制、中子星自转与磁场演化等，需要进一步理论研究。

Abstract: Analysis of observational data and theoretical modeling favors a bimodal distribution of the natal velocity kick of neutron stars. For $\sim200$ normal isolated radio pulsars with well-determined spin and kinematic parameters, we determine if they belong to the low- or high-velocity mode of the distribution. Our results demonstrate that about $30\%$ belong to the low-velocity mode. We then analyze the differences in the properties of the two sets of pulsars. For some parameters (characteristic ages, distances, and radio luminosities), we see a clear difference between the two modes. However, for these quantities, it can be easily attributed to selection bias. For those parameters that are not a subject of strong selection, such as pulse width, we do not observe any difference. Interestingly, we detect a significant difference in the magnetic field distribution between the two modes. Lower field pulsars ($B\lesssim 10^{12}$~G) are overabundant among objects from the low-velocity mode in comparison to the high-velocity one. Among pulsars with low field ($\lesssim 10^{11}$~G), we do not identify any objects from the high-velocity mode of the kick distribution. The origin of this discrepancy is not clear, and we discuss several possibilities.

</details>


### [148] [Evidence for candidate X-ray pulsations from the ultraluminous X-ray source NGC 7456 ULX-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20308)
*Yuanle Yao,Xiang-Dong Li,Xiao-Jie Xu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 在NGC7456 ULX-1（一个超亮X射线源）中检测到约0.22 Hz的候选脉动信号，通过多种时序分析技术识别，估计表面偶极磁场强度为10^12-10^14 G，表明该源可能包含吸积中子星。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确认NGC7456 ULX-1是否包含吸积中子星，以区分ULX的致密天体类型（中子星vs黑洞），并理解超Eddington吸积过程。

Method: 利用2023年XMM-Newton观测数据，采用加速搜索、Z^2_n统计量、轨道解调分析等多种独立时序技术来检测脉动信号并恢复相位相干性。

Result: 检测到约0.22 Hz的候选脉动信号，频率在观测期间发生漂移（表明吸积扭矩驱动的快速自转演化），估计表面偶极磁场B~10^12-10^14 G。

Conclusion: NGC7456 ULX-1可能包含吸积中子星，但需独立数据集或额外观测进行确认。

Abstract: We report evidence for a candidate pulsational signal at $\sim0.22$~Hz from NGC7456 ULX-1, a previously identified ultraluminous X-ray source (ULX). The signal is identified in the 2023 XMM-Newton observation using independent timing techniques including accelerated searches, $Z^2_n$ statistics, and an orbital-demodulation analysis designed to restore phase coherence in the presence of binary motion. The candidate pulsation frequency drift within the observation suggests rapid spin evolution driven by accretion torque. We further estimate the surface dipole magnetic field strength to be $B\sim 10^{12}-10^{14}$ G. These results provide evidence that NGC7456 ULX-1 may host an accreting neutron star, although confirmation with independent datasets or additional observations is required.

</details>

### [149] [The ESSnuSB Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19391)
*Monojit Ghosh*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ESSnuSBplus（ESSnuSB项目第一阶段）的物理计划，包括利用长基线中微子振荡实验在第二振荡最大处高精度测量$δ_{\rm CP}$，以及利用大型远距离探测器开展太阳、大气和超新星中微子研究，并增设低能量监测束流和低能量nuSTORM设施用于截面测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ESSnuSBplus旨在通过第二振荡最大处的高精度$δ_{\rm CP}$测量，解决中微子CP破坏这一基本物理问题，同时扩展中微子物理研究范围，包括天体物理中微子观测和截面测量。

Method: 采用ESSnuSB长基线中微子振荡实验平台，利用大型远距离探测器进行束流中微子、太阳中微子、大气中微子和超新星中微子的多物理研究；phase-I阶段增设低能量监测束流和低能量nuSTORM设施进行截面测量。

Result: 预期在第二振荡最大处以前所未有的精度测量CP破坏相位$δ_{\rm CP}$，并同时获得天体物理中微子和中微子-核子相互作用截面的测量结果。

Conclusion: ESSnuSBplus实验方案具有多维物理目标，既能实现高精度CP破坏测量，又能拓展中微子天体物理和相互作用研究，是欧洲中微子物理的重要前沿项目。

Abstract: In this proceedings, we will describe the physics program of the ESSnuSBplus, phase-I of the ESSnuSB project. ESSnuSB is a future long-baseline neutrino oscillation experiment in Europe which aims to measure $δ_{\rm CP}$ at the second oscillation maximum with with unprecedented precision. Apart from studying the beam based physics, the large far detector is also capable of studying various other physics cases involving solar, atmopsheric and supernova neutrinos. Under the ESSnuSBplus project, there will be a low energy monitored beam and a low energy nuSTORM facility for the measurement of cross-section.

</details>


### [150] [Towards Engineering Scaling Laws with Pretraining Data Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19781)
*Jan-Lucas Uslu,Kevin Greif,Daniel Whiteson,Benjamin Nachman*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究了粒子物理中大型模型的神经缩放定律，发现与语言模型类似，性能随计算量、模型大小和数据量呈幂律缩放。但与自然语言不同，基础物理可低成本生成高保真合成数据，这有利于数据多于参数的缩放模式。通过在喷注分类任务中引入更多样化、与下游任务更对齐的预训练数据，可以引导缩放行为向需要更多数据而非更大模型的方向发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究粒子物理领域大型模型的神经缩放定律，特别是与语言模型的异同，以及如何利用物理领域高保真模拟器的优势来引导缩放行为。

Method: 在高能粒子束碰撞产生的强子喷注分类任务上，通过引入更丰富多样且与下游分类任务更对齐的预训练数据，研究缩放行为的变化。

Result: 通过增加预训练数据的多样性和与下游任务的对齐程度，可以引导缩放行为向需要更多数据而非更大模型参数的方向发展。

Conclusion: 物理领域由于可以低成本生成高保真合成数据，能够通过数据工程主动引导缩放行为，实现数据驱动而非参数驱动的性能提升。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws describe how model performance improves as a power law in compute, model size, and dataset size. While well-established for large language models, these relationships are emerging for large models in particle physics. As with language, empirical studies show that the performance scales as a power law. However, unlike natural language or image domains, fundamental physics has high-fidelity simulators that produce synthetic data cheaply. This favors scaling regimes where additional data is cheaper than additional parameters, and allows the pretraining dataset itself to be engineered to influence the scaling. For the task of classifying hadronic jets produced in collisions of high-energy particle beams, we show that the scaling behavior can be engineered towards requiring more data rather than larger models by inclusion of pretraining data which is more diverse and better aligned with the downstream classification task.

</details>


### [151] [Resonant heterodyne conversion applied to a low-frequency haloscope for dark matter axion searches in the 1-35 MHz range](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20422)
*Navarro-Madrid Jose R.,Reina-Valero José,Díaz-Morcillo Alejandro,Gimeno Benito*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用RADES-BabyIAXO谐振腔中的共振外差上转换技术，通过微波腔搜索低质量暗物质轴子，推导了轴子电动力学源项及功率提取公式，识别了最优模式对，并在低温铜和超导铌腔条件下，预计在90%置信水平下可探测到低至$10^{-15}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$的轴子-光子耦合系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低质量暗物质轴子难以通过传统方法探测，需要发展新的共振外差上转换技术以提高灵敏度。现有方法在带宽、泵漏抑制和模式匹配方面存在不足，亟需建立更精确的理论模型和实验方案。

Method: 从轴子电动力学出发推导轴子诱导源项和读出模式提取功率，引入有效品质因子描述泵-轴子混合、检测带宽和信号功率；将BI-RME 3D全波公式扩展到双端口腔的外差轴子探测，考虑泵漏进入读出通道；分析RADES-BabyIAXO腔中最优模式对$\mathrm{quasi\textrm{-}TE}_{011}-\mathrm{quasi\textrm{-}TM}_{010}$；推导最优端口耦合以最大化扫描速率。

Result: 识别出$\mathrm{quasi\textrm{-}TE}_{011}-\mathrm{quasi\textrm{-}TM}_{010}$模式对为最优配置，可覆盖0.9–34.6 MHz轴子频率范围；解析解与全波模拟在谐振时高度一致，全波模型在非谐振时更精确，并能表征泵漏；在热噪声限制和充分泵漏抑制条件下，低温铜腔和超导铌腔可达到$10^{-15}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$的轴子-光子耦合灵敏度。

Conclusion: 所提出的共振外差上转换方案在RADES-BabyIAXO实验中有望显著提升低质量轴子搜索灵敏度，优于此前外差探测结果，为未来轴子暗物质探测提供了有效技术路线。

Abstract: We study resonant heterodyne up-conversion in the RADES-BabyIAXO haloscope as a method to search for low-mass dark matter axions using microwave cavities. Starting from axion electrodynamics, we derive the axion-induced source term and the power extracted through a readout mode, explicitly accounting for the finite axion linewidth. This leads to effective quality factors that determine the pump-axion mixing, detection bandwidth, and detected signal power. We extend the BI-RME 3D full-wave formulation to heterodyne axion detection in a realistic two-port cavity, including pump leakage into the readout channel. Applying the formalism to the largest RADES-BabyIAXO cavity identifies the $\mathrm{quasi\textrm{-}TE}_{011}-\mathrm{quasi\textrm{-}TM}_{010}$ mode pair as a favorable configuration, enabling sensitivity to axion frequencies between 0.9 and 34.6 MHz. Analytical and full-wave predictions show excellent agreement at resonance, while the full-wave model provides a more accurate description off resonance and allows a precise characterization of the pump leakage. We also derive the optimal port couplings that maximize the scanning rate. Sensitivity projections for cryogenic copper and superconducting niobium cavities indicate that, under thermal-noise-limited conditions and assuming sufficient pump-leakage rejection, the experiment could probe axion-photon couplings down to $10^{-15}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$ at 90% confidence level, representing a significant improvement over previous heterodyne-based searches.

</details>


### [152] [HEPTv2: End-to-End Efficient Point Transformer for Charged Particle Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20437)
*Siqi Miao,Shitij Govil,Jack P. Rodgers,Mia Liu,Javier Duarte,Shih-Chieh Hsu,Yuan-Tang Chou,Pan Li*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: HEPTv2是一种端到端的点变换器架构，用于高能物理中的带电粒子径迹重建，无需图构建或聚类等辅助阶段。在TrackML数据集上，它以98.6%的效率、0.8%的假阳性率、约15ms推理时间和0.4GB峰值内存实现了最先进的精度-延迟权衡，相比此前最优的基于图和变换器的方法在效率和延迟上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高亮度大型强子对撞机(HL-LHC)的极高碰撞密度要求径迹重建算法在保持精确高效的同时，还需应对极端的组合歧义。现有基于图神经网络的方法受限于图构建和处理的巨大开销，而变换器方法依赖辅助阶段，无法实现端到端优化。

Method: HEPTv2采用端到端点变换器架构，包含：(1) 基于局部敏感哈希(LSH)的局部感知点编码器，在探测器坐标空间中保持几何信息并实现高效局部注意力；(2) 径迹解码器，通过扇区化解码和直接命中-径迹预测，在联合编码器-解码器监督下实现端到端优化，无需图构建、聚类或过滤。

Result: 在TrackML数据集上，HEPTv2达到98.6%的双多数径迹效率、0.8%的假阳性率，推理时间约15ms，峰值内存0.4GB(NVIDIA A100)。延迟和内存随事件命中数近似线性扩展(最高5×10^5命中)。相比此前最优变换器方法效率提升4.5%，延迟降低7倍；相比优化后的图方法效率提升1.1-2.2%，延迟降低38-52倍。

Conclusion: HEPTv2证明了端到端变换器能够在HL-LHC所需的精度和效率下实现实时粒子径迹重建，在精度-延迟权衡上树立了新的最先进水平。

Abstract: Charged-particle tracking -- reconstructing trajectories from sparse detector measurements -- is a fundamental high-energy-physics inference problem and a canonical example of learning under extreme combinatorial ambiguity. At the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider (HL-LHC), tracking must remain accurate and efficient despite unprecedented collision densities. Graph neural networks perform strongly, but incur substantial costs from graph construction and processing, while transformer-based approaches rely on auxiliary stages that prevent end-to-end optimization. To address this, we present HEPTv2, an end-to-end point-transformer architecture that reconstructs tracks from detector hits in one trainable pipeline. HEPTv2 combines a locality-aware point encoder with a track decoder that predicts complete trajectories without graph-building, clustering, or filtering. The encoder uses locality-sensitive hashing in detector coordinate space to preserve tracking-relevant geometry while enabling efficient local attention. The decoder resolves ambiguities through sectorized decoding and direct hit-to-track prediction under joint encoder-decoder supervision, allowing the full pipeline to be optimized end-to-end. On TrackML, HEPTv2 achieves 98.6% double-majority tracking efficiency at a 0.8% fake rate, while requiring only $\sim$15~ms inference time and 0.4~GB peak memory per event on a NVIDIA A100 GPU. Latency and memory scale approximately linearly for events with up to $5\times10^5$ hits. HEPTv2 establishes a new state of the art in the accuracy-latency trade-off, improving efficiency by 4.5% over the strongest prior transformer and by 1.1--2.2% over optimized graph-based pipelines, while reducing latency by factors of 7 and 38--52, respectively. These results show end-to-end transformers can deliver the accuracy and efficiency required for real-time particle reconstruction at the HL-LHC.

</details>


### [153] [Observation of electroweak production of pairs of Z bosons in proton-proton collisions at 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20558)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS实验首次报道了在质子-质子对撞中电弱产生的一对Z玻色子并伴随两个喷注的证据，在138 fb$^{-1}$的13 TeV数据中测得电弱产生截面为0.37$^{+0.14}_{-0.12}$(stat)$^{+0.06}_{-0.06}$(syst) fb，与标准模型预测0.39 ± 0.06 fb吻合，信号显著度3.1个标准差，联合四轻子道后显著度达5.0个标准差，并给出了反常四规范玻色子耦合的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证标准模型中电弱产生ZZjj过程的预言，并搜寻可能超出标准模型的新物理信号，特别是反常四规范玻色子耦合。

Method: 利用CMS探测器在质心能量13 TeV、积分亮度138 fb$^{-1}$的质子-质子对撞数据，选取同味反号双轻子、大横动量缺失以及大快度间隔和大不变质量的双喷注事件，通过拟合提取电弱产生信号，并与标准模型理论预测进行比较。

Result: 电弱产生截面σ_EW(pp → ZZjj → ℓℓννjj) = 0.37^{+0.14}_{-0.12} (stat)^{+0.06}_{-0.06} (syst) fb，信号显著度3.1个标准差（预期2.8），与标准模型预言0.39 ± 0.06 fb一致。联合四轻子道后观测（预期）显著度达5.0（4.5）个标准差。给出了反常四规范玻色子耦合的限制。

Conclusion: 首次获得了ZZjj电弱产生的实验证据，截面测量与标准模型一致，联合分析首次实现了Z玻色子对电弱产生的5σ观测，并对反常四规范玻色子耦合给出了新的实验限制。

Abstract: The first evidence of electroweak (EW) production of pairs of Z bosons in association with two jets (jj) in the final state ZZjj $\to$ $\ell\ellνν$jj, where $\ell$ = e, $μ$, is reported by the CMS experiment. The analysis is based on a data sample of proton-proton (pp) collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. Events are selected by requiring exactly two leptons of same flavor and opposite charge, large missing transverse momentum, and two jets with a large rapidity separation and large invariant mass. The EW production cross section in a fiducial volume is $σ_{\mathrm{EW}}$(pp $\to$ ZZjj $\to$ $\ell\ellνν$jj) = 0.37$^{+0.14}_{-0.12}$ (stat)$^{+0.06}_{-0.06}$ (syst) fb, in agreement with the standard model prediction of 0.39 $\pm$ 0.06 fb. The observed (expected) significance of the signal is 3.1 (2.8) standard deviations. Limits on anomalous quartic gauge couplings are derived in terms of dimension-8 effective field theory operators. A combination with the previously reported result from the ZZ decay channel with four charged leptons yields an observed (expected) significance of 5.0 (4.5) standard deviations for the EW production of Z boson pairs.

</details>

### [154] [West Nile virus outbreak in Italy modelled with the quantum Game of Life](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20489)
*Andrea Fontana,Simone Tambascia,Ciro Di Carluccio,Andrea Esposito,Bernardo Spagnolo,Andrea M. Chiariello*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子生命游戏元胞自动机模型，用于模拟2025年夏季意大利西尼罗病毒疫情的传播，通过优化蚊虫出生率和清除率参数，实现了对局部和区域层面累计感染病例曲线的高精度拟合，并量化了环境变化和防控措施对疫情传播的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来意大利WNV感染异常高发，需要一种能够描述病毒传播动态、评估环境变化和防控措施影响的量化工具，以支持决策者制定监测和控制策略。

Method: 采用量子版本的Game of Life元胞自动机模型，人类动力学按照GOL规则演化，蚊虫作为疾病载体引入随机动力学过程，同时模拟人与蚊虫的交互作用，仅优化蚊虫出生率和清除率两个参数。

Result: 模型在局部和区域平均层面均能以高精度拟合累计感染病例曲线；通过调整参数值，量化了蚊虫防控措施和因气候/生态变化导致的蚊虫数量激增对疫情传播的影响。

Conclusion: 该模型提供了意大利WNV感染传播的通用定量描述，可作为测试不同环境情景的支持工具，有助于决策者制定监测疾病载体动态和控制病毒传播的策略。

Abstract: In the last years, an anomalously high spreading of West Nile virus (WNV) has been observed in Italy, with particularly high peaks of infections in southern Lazio, Campania and Veneto regions. The main disease vector for WNV is represented by Culex pipiens mosquitoes, which spread human infections through their bites. Here, we investigate WNV fever epidemic diffusion during summer season 2025 in Italy through a computational approach based on a quantum version of the Game of Life (GOL) cellular automaton model. Specifically, human dynamics evolves according to the GOL rules, while stochastic dynamics of disease vectors, i.e., mosquitoes, as well as their interaction with humans, simultaneously occur. We show that this model fits the curves of cumulative infected individuals with high accuracy, either at local and average-regional level, with only optimization of mosquito birth and removal rates parameters. Furthermore, leveraging model flexibility, we show that changes in model parameters values elucidate system response to environmental variations. For instance, we quantify, e.g., the impact of mosquito spreading containment measures or sudden mosquito increasing abundance due to climatic and ecological changes. Overall, we provide a general, quantitative description of WNV infection spreading in Italy which could represent a supportive tool to test different environmental scenarios and could be useful to devise strategies for decision makers to monitor disease vector dynamics and to control consequent virus diffusion.

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Covariate-Adjusted Functional Principal Components Analysis for Modeling Hazard Rates of Physical Activity in the US Population](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19760)
*Md Rokibul Hasan,Pratim Guha Niyogi*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于风险函数的分布分析方法，利用手腕加速度计数据建模个体活动-强度模式，通过功能主成分分析描述身体活动强度分布的异质性，优于传统均值摘要方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 身体活动在人类健康中至关重要，但其整体分布在不同人群中存在差异。常用的摘要指标无法描述这种分布模式，因此需要一种基于分布的分析方法来更好地刻画身体活动的强度分布特征。

Method: 利用2011-2012年NHANES中4297名成年人连续7天的分钟级MIMS数据，通过生存分析法为每个个体在公共强度网格上推导出非参数活动-强度风险函数，将风险曲线及其对数变换作为功能对象，并采用功能主成分分析（FPCA）表征活动-强度分布的主要变异模式。

Result: 组均值风险函数在低强度水平上差异较小，而在高强度水平上存在显著差异。基于风险的功能表示能灵活且可解释地捕捉个体间身体活动强度分布的异质性，优于基于均值的摘要方法。

Conclusion: 基于风险的功能表示方法能有效刻画身体活动强度分布差异，支持不同人群亚组间身体活动模式的原则性比较，是一种灵活且可解释的分析框架。

Abstract: Physical activity plays a vital role in human health. Its entire distribution differs among people. Commonly used summary measures cannot describe this distributional pattern. We present a distribution-based analytical approach to describe physical activity by modeling individual-level activity-intensity patterns through hazard functions derived from wrist-worn accelerometer data. We analyzed minute-level Monitor-Independent Movement Summary (MIMS) data of 4297 adults with seven continuous days of device wear from the 2011- 2012 National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey (NHANES). We derived a nonparametric activity-intensity hazard using a survival-based approach for each individual on a common intensity grid, treating both the hazard curves from MIMS and their log-transformed MIMS as functional objects. We used functional principal component analysis (FPCA) on both scales of MIMS to characterize dominant modes of variation in activity-intensity distributions. Group-wise mean hazard functions showed little difference at lower intensity levels, while we observed a substantial difference at higher intensity levels. Our results demonstrate that hazard-based functional representations for capturing differences in physical activity intensity distributions across individuals offer a flexible and interpretable way to characterize heterogeneity. This approach works better than mean-based summaries and supports principled comparisons of physical activity patterns across population subgroups.

</details>
